*Diab W. Abueidda,Bilal Ahmed,Panos Pantidis,Mostafa E. Mobasher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PA-SciML框架，通过可核验的物理约束检查（如边界条件、因果性等）来审计AI代理发现的科学机器学习代理模型，而不仅仅依赖误差指标。在固体力学案例中，该方法能选出符合物理规律的代理模型，而纯误差基线可能违反因果性等物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM代理的科学机器学习方法仅依赖误差指标选择代理模型，但低误差不能保证预测场满足力学中重要的物理约束（如边界条件、叠加原理、刚度缩放、因果性等），导致模型可能物理上不可信。

Method: PA-SciML采用"验证优先"工作流：在搜索前固定评分评估器；推导可审查的机器可核验物理需求；对每个候选模型进行输出检查；在无参考解的情况下，分别在规定输入范围或实测载荷历史跨度内搜索高违例情况；仅当通过所述检查后才报告模型已验证。此外，可在训练前添加数值探针，并一次只测试一个建模变更以记录孤立编辑与分数提升的关联。

Result: 在静态弹性算例中，PA-SciML选出的代理模型验证误差低于纯误差基线，且两者均通过线性弹性检查；在瞬态弹动力学算例中，纯误差基线虽误差相近但未能通过严格的因果性检查（对未来载荷部分有响应），而PA-SciML选出的模型通过了所有检查。

Conclusion: PA-SciML的核心区别在于对每个候选模型提供预测场上的物理证据，而非仅提供更丰富的聚合分数；验证优先的框架能有效确保代理模型同时满足误差指标和关键物理约束。

Abstract: In agentic scientific machine learning (SciML), large language model (LLM) agents can discover surrogate models and select one by an automated score, typically an error metric. A low error, however, does not establish that the predicted fields satisfy the physics that matter for mechanics, such as boundary conditions, superposition, stiffness scaling, or causality. We introduce Physics-Audited Agentic SciML (PA-SciML), a verification-first workflow for agentic SciML discovery. The workflow fixes a scoring evaluator before search, derives reviewable machine-checkable physics requirements, checks each trained candidate on its outputs, and separately searches prescribed input ranges or measured load-history spans for high-violation cases without reference solution fields. A surrogate is reported as verified only under the stated checks. When enabled, the workflow also adds advisory numerical probes before training and tests one modeling change at a time to record which isolated edits are associated with score gains before reuse. In the reported computational-solid-mechanics numerical examples, the static elasticity run selects a surrogate with lower validation error than the error-only baseline while both selected models pass the common linear-elastic checks. In the transient elastodynamics run, an error-only baseline with similar mean error fails a stricter causality check by responding to future parts of the loading history, while the selected surrogate passes the stated checks. The main distinction is per-candidate physics evidence on predicted fields, not a richer aggregate score.

</details>


### [36] [MIRA-Math: A Benchmark for Minimal Information Requesting and Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07391)
*Charbel Al Bateh,Samer Saab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA-Math是一个数学推理基准测试，要求模型在缺失一个关键事实的情况下，通过自然语言请求获取缺失信息，并在严格预算下整合该事实得出唯一正确答案。包含2,310个实例，覆盖22种数学类型。实验发现请求成功与最终答案准确性是可分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准通常提供所有必要事实，而交互式基准则混合了推理与工具使用、检索和长程对话。本文旨在填补一个更狭窄的诊断能力缺口：评估模型在缺少一个必要原子事实的情况下，通过主动请求获取缺失信息并完成精确推理的能力。

Method: (1) 构建MIRA-Math基准，每个问题具有唯一答案，但求解视角恰好缺失一个必要原子事实。(2) 使用固定约束LLM作为响应器，仅提供数据集中的原子事实，当请求匹配时返回该事实，否则拒绝。(3) 实例生成、类型化提示规范、验证和最终答案验证均为确定性的。(4) 在22种数学类型上生成2,310个实例，涵盖代数、概率、线性系统、离散结构、信号处理、马尔可夫链、电路、插值和数值边值问题。

Result: 实验表明，请求成功和最终答案准确性是可分离的：模型可能请求了正确事实但后续计算失败，也可能在获得规范提示之前就失败。跨前沿模型和小模型的实验结果支持这一发现。

Conclusion: MIRA-Math提供了一个可复现的基准，用于评估数学推理中最小信息请求能力。作者发布了生成器、验证器、提示词、运行元数据和数据集文档，以支持可复现的评估。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning benchmarks typically provide all facts needed to solve each problem, while interactive benchmarks often mix reasoning with tools, retrieval, and long-horizon dialogue. We introduce MIRA-Math, a benchmark for a narrower diagnostic capability: solving mathematical problems whose full latent state has a unique answer, but whose solver-facing view is missing exactly one necessary atomic fact. The solver must request the missing information in natural language under a strict budget and then integrate the returned fact into an exact final answer. A fixed constrained LLM responder sees only the dataset-provided atomic fact and must either offer the quoted fact when the request matches it, or decline otherwise. Thus, instance generation, typed hint specifications, validation, and final-answer verification are deterministic, while request metrics are measured under a fixed LLM-mediated responder channel. MIRA-Math contains 2{,}310 generated instances from 22 typed mathematical families spanning algebra, probability, linear systems, discrete structures, signal processing, Markov chains, circuits, interpolation, and numerical boundary-value problems. Experiments across frontier and small models show that request success and final-answer accuracy are separable: models may ask for the right fact yet fail the downstream computation, or fail before obtaining the canonical hint. We release generators, verifiers, prompts, run metadata, and dataset documentation to support reproducible evaluation of minimal information requesting in mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [37] [Agentic Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07397)
*Elaine Ang,Chenxi Huang,Georgios Liargkovas,Jerry Liu,Jinhui Liu,Nikos Pagonas,Charlie Summers,Haonan Wang,Jiakai Xu,Tianle Zhou,Yusen Zhang,Zhou Yu,Zhuo Zhang,Tianyi Peng,Kostis Kaffes,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"智能体数据环境"（Agentic Data Environments）的概念，将数据系统从被动的状态存储重新定义为智能体安全、可靠执行的主动基础，旨在平衡自动化收益与失败风险控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在速度、规模和劳动效率上具有显著优势，但其失败往往带来突然且不可逆的成本。核心挑战在于在提升自动化收益的同时，约束失败后果的边界。

Method: 提出Agentic Data Environments概念，将数据系统重构为智能体运行的执行基质，使其既能增强智能体能力，又能强制执行安全保障。

Result: 初步工作成果展示了如何将数据系统从被动的状态存储转变为智能体安全可靠执行的主动基础。

Conclusion: 数据系统需要超越传统数据库的范畴，覆盖文件、API、应用和系统状态等更广泛的数据环境，从而为智能体自动化提供既增强能力又保障安全的基础设施。

Abstract: Autonomous agents promise substantial gains in speed, scale, and labor efficiency, but their failures can impose abrupt and often irreversible costs. The central challenge for agentic automation is therefore to increase the benefits of automation while bounding the consequences of failure.
  While databases remain central to modern computing, agents operate over a broader data environment spanning files, APIs, applications, and system state. In this talk, I will outline early work on Agentic Data Environments -- the execution substrate in which agents operate -- that both amplify agent capabilities and enforce safety guarantees. This perspective reframes data systems from passive stores of state into active substrates for safe, reliable execution.

</details>


### [38] [Reason Less, Verify More: Deterministic Gates Recover a Silent Policy-Violation Failure Mode in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07405)
*Vikas Reddy,Sumanth Reddy Challaram,Abhishek Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM agent在政策宽松环境中执行工具调用时产生的“静默错误状态”问题（即工具执行了违反策略的操作但未产生报错），并在航空领域τ²-bench基准上发现78%的失败源于此类静默错误。提出了一种轻量级干预措施：确定性只读预执行门控（pre-execution gates），在工具写入前检查调用合法性和当前状态。四门控套件在gpt-4o-mini上将成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4pp，p=0.0012），并在另一个15种子集上复现（+12.3pp，p=0.0008）。效果集中体现在门控触发的26/50个任务上（+19.2pp），未触发门控的24个任务无显著变化。负对照实验（零售领域和BFCL）表明门控仅在工具策略宽松时有效。在gpt-5.2前沿模型上同样观察到此类失败模式，门控将成功率从61.2%提升至71.6%（+10.4pp，p=0.020，未复现）。贡献在于提供了有界的评估和可靠性结果：确定性门控不能保证任务成功，但能在动作边界上确定性阻止一类已知的静默策略违规写入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在使用工具时可能在策略宽松环境下执行违反策略的操作，且不产生任何错误信号，形成“静默错误状态”（silent wrong state）。这种失败模式难以被检测和防御，威胁agent系统的安全性。现有研究缺乏对这种失败模式的系统性量化分析及有效干预手段。

Method: 在τ²-bench航空领域基准上量化静默错误状态的失败率，提出确定性只读预执行门控（pre-execution gates）作为轻量级干预：在工具执行写入操作前，检查调用合法性和当前状态，若不满足策略则阻止写入。设计了四个门控组成的套件，并在gpt-4o-mini和gpt-5.2上评估。使用负对照实验（零售领域和BFCL）验证机制边界。

Result: 在预算agent上，78%的失败为静默错误状态失败（无工具报错），且失败率在不同随机种子间可复现。四门控套件在gpt-4o-mini上全基准成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4pp，p=0.0012），15种子复现集提升+12.3pp（p=0.0008）。效果集中在门控触发的26/50任务上（+19.2pp），非触发任务无显著变化。负对照显示门控仅在工具策略宽松时有效。gpt-5.2上成功率从61.2%提升至71.6%（+10.4pp，p=0.020，未复现）。

Conclusion: 确定性预执行门控不能保证任务成功，但能确定性阻止一类已知的静默策略违规写入。提供了有界的评估和可靠性结果，并建议此类失败模式在前沿模型上仍然存在。贡献在于识别并量化了静默错误状态问题，并提出了一种轻量级、可解释、可复现的防御机制。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents can violate the very policies they are deployed to enforce while appearing to complete the task successfully. In policy-permissive environments, a tool may execute any well-formed call even when the corresponding state transition is forbidden by domain policy. The result is a silent wrong state (a booking cancelled, a passenger count changed, a claim acted on without verification) that neither the tool nor the agent's self-report exposes.
  We study this failure mode in the $τ^2$-bench airline domain. On a budget agent, 78% of observed failures are silent wrong-state failures with no tool error, and the aggregate failure rate is reproducible across disjoint seeds, not sampling noise. We then evaluate a lightweight intervention: deterministic, read-only pre-execution gates that inspect the proposed call and current state before allowing a write. A four-gate suite raises full-benchmark success from 29.6% to 42.0% on gpt-4o-mini (+12.4pp; paired task-level bootstrap P=0.0012), and the lift reproduces on a disjoint 15-seed set (+12.3pp; P=0.0008).
  The effect is concentrated where the gates fire: on the 26/50 firing tasks, success rises by +19.2pp, while movement on the 24 non-firing tasks does not exclude zero. Two negative controls (a self-enforcing retail domain and BFCL) bound the mechanism: gates help when tools are policy-permissive and add little where tools already self-enforce. As suggestive evidence, not a central claim, the same failure mode persists at the frontier: gpt-5.2 at default reasoning still attempts policy-violating writes, and the same suite improves success from 61.2% to 71.6% (+10.4pp; P=0.020; n=5, no replication). The contribution is a bounded evaluation and reliability result: deterministic gates do not guarantee task success, but they can deterministically prevent a known class of silent policy-violating writes at the action boundary.

</details>


### [39] [InductWave: Inductive Multi-Hop Logical Query Answering on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07422)
*Mayank Kharbanda,Michael Cochez,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InductWave，一种基于小波变换的归纳式嵌入方法，用于知识图谱上的逻辑多跳查询回答。该方法在训练图节点少于测试图的条件下，使用更少的消息传递层（仅需基线模型75%的层数）即可达到或超越现有模型性能，并能扩展至大规模图谱如Wiki-KG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱逻辑查询方法主要基于EFO查询和直推式推理，无法处理训练中未见的实体。现实世界中资源稀缺，无法在大型KG的所有节点上训练模型，因此需要一种归纳式、低资源消耗的查询推理方法。

Method: 提出InductWave，一种基于小波变换的归纳式嵌入方法。训练图包含比测试图更少的节点，通过小波变换实现归纳推理，并使用更少的消息传递层（仅为基线模型的50%-75%）。

Result: 在与基线模型同等性能下仅需一半的消息传递层数；在大多数情况下使用75%的层数即可超越所有基线模型。低资源需求使其能够扩展到Wiki-KG等大规模图谱。在FB15k-237数据集上进行了不同训练-测试图比例的大量实验验证。

Conclusion: InductWave通过小波变换实现高效归纳式逻辑查询回答，显著降低了资源需求（更少的消息传递层），同时保持或超越现有方法性能，能够有效应用于大规模知识图谱。

Abstract: Logical Multi-Hop Query Answering over Knowledge Graphs (KGs) can be formulated as querying, with an implicit completeness assumption. Current works mainly focus on Existential First Order Logic (EFO) queries. These EFO queries contain conjunction, disjunction, and negation operators. Most existing works employ transductive reasoning, meaning they are not capable of reasoning over entities unseen during training. In the real world, there is a resource scarcity, and we cannot train a model with all the nodes of a large KG. Hence, we propose InductWave, a wavelet-based inductive embedding method for logical query answering on large KGs. Here, the training graph consists of fewer nodes than the test graph. Our model performs on par with the baseline models while having half the number of message-passing layers. It outperforms all of them in most cases, with 75% of the layers. These fewer resource requirements enable us to evaluate InductWave on massive graphs, such as Wiki-KG. We test our model using extensive experiments across varying train-test graph proportions of the FB15k-(237) dataset, comparing it with the state-of-the-art models. The code and datasets for the model are available at https://github.com/kracr/inductwave/.

</details>


### [40] [The Blind Curator: How a Biased Judge Silently Disables Skill Retirement in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07436)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自进化系统中，当使用有偏的LLM作为裁判时，“技能退休”机制会悄然失效。通过有偏奖励分析和行为实验，发现“假阳性”（错误通过）偏差会彻底禁用基于贡献的技能退休机制，且该机制故障无法通过增加数据量来弥补。虽然下游性能可能表面稳定，但实际裁判已失能。文章贡献了一个行为安全结论，并提出了一种廉价的缺陷注入审计方法来预判裁判可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体依赖技能退休机制来剔除低质量技能，但该机制假设裁判是无偏的。在参考免费任务中，LLM裁判存在固有偏差，打破了这一假设。现有研究未充分揭示有偏裁判对技能退休机制的破坏机理，特别是偏差如何影响自我进化系统的安全性。

Method: 通过有偏奖励理论分析（corrupted-reward analysis）和行为实验（behavioral study）相结合。在确定性奖励基础上注入噪声，在参考免费的报告生成测试平台上进行代码生成交叉验证，隔离因果通道。区分真正的技能退休与帽式驱逐（cap-eviction churn）来定位机制故障。

Result: 对称噪声不影响退休机制；但假阳性偏差（错误通过）会在某个阈值后完全禁用基于贡献的技能退休，且无法通过增加数据解决。该机制故障跨领域、跨失败率普遍存在，仅在接近零假阳性的验证器式裁判中可避免。下游性能退化仅当偏差同时毒化技能合成时才会出现，否则表面稳定，形成“沉默失能”。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是一个行为安全结论而非性能结论：有偏裁判会悄然使自进化系统的自我监管机制失效。提出了一种廉价的缺陷注入审计方法，可在部署前判断裁判是否处于危险阈值一侧，为系统安全提供预警。

Abstract: A self-evolving agent retires its bad skills by watching them fail, so what happens when the judge cannot see the failures? Skill retirement is the structural constraint that keeps a growing library from drifting below the no-skill baseline, but its guarantee assumes an unbiased reward, which is false for the LLM judges that reference-free tasks force upon us. We show that a biased judge does not merely add noise; it \emph{silently switches off the curator}. We make this precise with a corrupted-reward analysis and, isolating the causal channel by injecting corruption on top of a deterministic reward, a behavioral study on a reference-free report-writing testbed with a code-generation cross-check. Symmetric noise leaves retirement intact, but \emph{false-pass} bias (failures slipping through as passes) disables contribution-based retirement past a sharp threshold that no amount of data can cross. Separating genuine retirement from cap-eviction churn shows this \emph{mechanism} failure is universal, holding across domains and failure rates and sparing only near-zero-false-pass, verifier-like graders. The downstream \emph{outcome}, though, is regime-dependent: eval quality degrades only where the same corruption also starves skill synthesis, and otherwise holds steady, so the disabled curator is \emph{silent}, surfacing in no aggregate metric. The contribution is a behavioral safety result, not a performance one. A cheap defect-injection audit then tells an operator, before deployment, which side of the threshold their judge occupies.

</details>


### [41] [SpaCellAgent: A Self-Evolving LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Trajectory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07467)
*Songhan Wang,Haoang Chi,He Li,Zhiheng Zhang,Jiayan Yuan,Cheems Wang,Hao Peng,Xinwang Liu,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpaCellAgent，一种基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，能够自动完成从自然语言指令到时空转录组学轨迹推断的全流程分析，在六个异构数据集上实现超过40%的效率提升，同时保持专家级分析水准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹推断方法需要大量手动干预和对多种异构工具的熟练使用，缺乏端到端的自动化方案，阻碍了高效的空间与单细胞转录组学轨迹分析。

Method: 采用多智能体架构进行策略性工作流规划，动态工具编排引擎实现自适应算法选择，自我进化模块通过反馈迭代优化性能，将自然语言规格说明转换为优化的分析工作流。

Result: 在六个异构数据集上（涵盖复杂时间发育轨迹、多种测序平台、空间分辨组织架构）进行验证，分析效率提升超过40%，性能与专家水平一致。

Conclusion: SpaCellAgent实现了高级时空建模的民主化，建立了可扩展的、智能体驱动的计算生物学新范式，代码和材料已开源。

Abstract: Spatial and Single-cell transcriptomics are transformative in deciphering cellular dynamics. As the fundamental paradigm for reconstructing cell developmental paths, trajectory inference (TI) is critical. However, existing methods require extensive manual intervention and proficiency in heterogeneous tools, posing a significant barrier to efficient TI analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose SpaCellAgent, an autonomous large language model (LLM) multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end spatiotemporal analysis and narrative generation. SpaCellAgent utilizes a multi-agent architecture for strategic workflow planning, a dynamic tool-orchestration engine for adaptive algorithm selection, and a self-evolution module that iteratively refines performance through feedback. We evaluate SpaCellAgent on six heterogeneous datasets encompassing complex temporal developmental trajectories, diverse sequencing platforms, and spatially-resolved tissue architectures. SpaCellAgent consistently demonstrates over 40\% improvement in analytical efficiency while maintaining expert-aligned performance. By converting natural language specifications into optimized analytical workflows and fully automating the pipeline, SpaCellAgent democratizes advanced spatiotemporal modeling and establishes a scalable, agent-driven paradigm for computational biology. The code and materials are available at https://github.com/LittleXH-shw/SpaCellAgent.

</details>


### [42] [Search, Fail, Recover: A Training Framework for Correction-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07492)
*Dmitry Beresnev,Vladimir Makharev,Roman Khalikov,Ivan Oseledets,Petr Anokhin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pyligent框架，通过显式监督失败分支（continue/finish/backtrack三种动作）来训练模型在推理中恢复错误，在隐藏图、数独和Blocksworld等任务上显著优于仅使用正确解微调的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多推理任务不能用简单的从左到右的链式过程来描述，求解器需要探索可行分支、发现延迟失败并回退到可继续的前缀。现有方法仅模仿正确解链，无法教会模型从错误中恢复。

Method: 提出Pyligent框架，受Diligent Learner启发，将推理表示为对部分解链的验证搜索。任务验证器标记生成continuation和失败，将搜索树转化为三种动作（continue、finish、backtrack）的有监督训练目标，并可选添加废弃分支的摘要轨迹。

Result: 在隐藏有向图任务上提升72.7个百分点；在混合/专家数独上提升17/18个百分点；在带推理轨迹的混合/专家数独上提升27/14个百分点；在Blocksworld上提升13个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式的失败分支监督可以教会模型有用的恢复行为，超越了仅模仿完整解链的效果。

Abstract: Many reasoning tasks are not well described by a single left-to-right chain: a solver may need to pursue a plausible branch, observe delayed failure, and return to the latest prefix that can still be completed. We introduce Pyligent, a training and inference framework inspired by the Diligent Learner formulation that represents reasoning as validated search over partial solution chains. A task validator labels generated continuations and failures, and the resulting search trees are converted into supervised targets for three actions: continue, finish, and backtrack, with optional traces that summarize abandoned branches. We evaluate Pyligent on a hidden directed graph task designed to isolate delayed-failure recovery, and on structured reasoning domains with exact validators, including $4{\times}4$ Sudoku, Sudoku with reasoning traces, and Blocksworld. Compared with gold-only supervised fine-tuning, Pyligent improves solve rate by $72.7$ percentage points on hidden graphs, by $17$ and $18$ points on mixed and expert Sudoku, by $27$ and $14$ points on mixed and expert Sudoku with reasoning traces, and by $13$ points on Blocksworld. These results suggest that explicit failed-branch supervision can teach useful recovery behavior beyond imitation of polished solution chains.

</details>


### [43] [Do LLM-Generated Skills Make Better AI Data Scientists? A Component Ablation Across Data-Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07504)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM生成的技能文件（skill files）是否能为数据科学任务带来性能提升。实验覆盖数据科学全生命周期四个阶段，共56个任务、9种模型配置、3个提供商，进行了7,560次运行。结果表明：完整的生成技能、以及所有消融变体，相比无技能基线均无显著性能提升（p值≥0.396，波动仅1.2 pp）；补充的token匹配控制实验（1,512次运行）进一步发现，完整技能与任务无关的技能格式内容表现相似。结论：将LLM生成的单个技能文件作为数据科学工作流的默认单次提示策略需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家编写的技能文件可封装高质量指导，但跨任务家族的手动编写与维护存在瓶颈。作者希望验证LLM自动生成的技能文件是否能作为一种低人工干预的替代方案，提升数据科学任务性能。

Method: 在数据准备、数据提取、统计分析、报告四个生命周期阶段，每个阶段使用一个LLM生成的技能文件。首先对比全技能与无技能基线；再通过消融实验（56任务×9模型配置×3提供商=7,560次运行）检验各技能组件的贡献；补充token匹配控制实验（1,512次运行）进一步验证技能内容的特异性。

Result: 完整的生成技能相比无技能提示无可靠提升（p≥0.396）；所有消融变体性能波动仅1.2个百分点；全技能与任务无关的技能格式内容表现相似。

Conclusion: LLM生成的单个技能文件作为数据科学工作流的默认单次提示策略效果不佳，不建议作为通用方案采用。

Abstract: Product data scientists often ask LLM-based agents to help with recurring execution tasks such as cleaning data, writing SQL, choosing statistical tests, and formatting results. Reusable skill files are meant to avoid prompting from scratch by packaging guidance for a task family. Expert-written skills can encode high-quality guidance, but writing and maintaining them across many data-science task families creates a manual bottleneck. We ask whether LLM-generated skills offer a useful low-curation alternative: do they improve performance over the task prompt alone? We test this question across four lifecycle stages: data preparation, data extraction, statistical analysis, and reporting, using one generated skill per stage. We find no reliable improvement from full generated skills over No-Skill prompting. We then ask whether any part of the skill is useful by ablating different skill components. The main ablation covers 56 tasks, nine model configurations, and three providers, yielding 7,560 runs. Compared with prompting using the task alone, neither the full generated skill nor any ablated skill variant significantly improves performance; all p-values are at least 0.396, and the total spread across variants is only 1.2 pp. A supplemental token-matched control adds 1,512 runs and finds that Full skills perform similarly to task-irrelevant skill-formatted content. The results caution against using one LLM-generated skill per data-science workflow as a default single-shot prompting strategy.

</details>


### [44] [RL Post-Training Builds Compositional Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07646)
*Azwar Abdulsalam,Nishil Patel,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究RL后训练能否将基模型中的原始技能组合成更高级的策略，在可观测的重写语法环境中，RL通过Trace推理任务使模型解决预训练模型难以解决的留出问题，并发现RL通过阶段性组合机制重组原始能力，形成可复用的顺序和并行组合策略，而拒绝微调表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RL后训练是否仅仅是放大基模型中已有的原始技能，还是能够将原始技能组合成新的高级策略。

Method: 在完全可观测的重写语法环境中，对Transformer进行原始符号重写链的预训练，然后在基于Trace的推理任务上使用仅含二元最终答案奖励的RL进行后训练，并通过Trace分析、拒绝微调对比、预训练消融实验等方法进行研究。

Result: RL解决了预训练模型即使在更大采样预算下也难以解决的留出问题；拒绝微调早期有提升但很快停滞；RL通过阶段性组合机制重组原始能力，形成顺序组合和并行组合等可复用的高级策略；RL与RFT的关键区别在于选择性而非探索量；组合策略的出现受预训练是否将原始能力组织为可压缩的约简程序所门控。

Conclusion: RL后训练能够将基模型中的弱程序性成分构建为可靠的高级策略，关键在于RL能够选择性探索有效的可复用结构，而预训练的组织方式对组合策略的涌现至关重要。

Abstract: Does RL post-training merely amplify primitive skills already latent in a base model, or can it compose primitive skills into new higher-level strategies? We study this question in a fully observable rewrite-grammar environment where the pretraining distribution is known and every generated rewrite can be audited. A Transformer is pretrained on primitive symbol-rewrite chains and post-trained on a Trace-based reasoning task with only a binary final-answer reward. RL solves held-out problems that remain rarely solved by the pretrained model even under much larger sampling budgets, while rejection fine-tuning improves early but plateaus. Trace analysis shows that RL reorganizes primitive competence through a phased compositional mechanism: it first strengthens primitive reductions, then discovers valid composed procedures. These include sequential compositions, which collapse ordered chains of primitive contractions, and parallel compositions, which combine independent primitive contractions in a single step. The composed procedures are not isolated samples; they are reused and consolidated into a stable repertoire. Comparing RL with rejection fine-tuning shows that the key difference is not exploration volume but selectivity: RFT produces many shortcut-like rewrites, much of them invalid, whereas RL concentrates exploration into valid reusable structure. Pretraining ablations show that the emergence of compositional strategies is gated not by primitive exposure alone, but by whether pretraining organizes primitive competence into reduction procedures that RL can later compress. The base model provides weak procedural ingredients; RL builds them into reliable higher-level strategies.

</details>


### [45] [Recursive Self-Improvement in AI: From Bounded Self-Refinement to Autonomous Research Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07663)
*Mingguang Chen,Licheng Wang,Bo Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对2024-2026年间1250篇arXiv论文进行了系统分类，沿两个维度分析AI自我改进：改进对象（行为、策略、评估器或研究过程）和闭环程度（人在回路到完全闭环）。提出"验证层次"概念，将自我评估信号从形式验证器（最强）到内在自我评估（最弱）排序，发现自我改进强度追踪该层次，失败模式源于其违反。指出"研究方向设定"瓶颈使人类保持在回路顶层，并将治理级测量自我改进定位为最被忽视的研究领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献使用"自我改进""自我奖励""自我对弈""自我进化"等词汇，混淆了根本不同的研究目标，缺乏系统性分类框架来区分有界的自我完善与开放式的递归自我改进（RSI），且缺乏对自我评估信号强度的统一分析。

Method: 调查1250篇arXiv论文（2024-2026），沿两个轴分类：改进对象（部署行为、训练策略、评估器、研究过程）和闭环程度（人在回路到完全闭环）。提出评估信号的设计空间（裁判、过程奖励模型、验证器、规则、元评估）并构建验证层次。

Result: 自我改进强度沿着验证层次递减（形式验证器最强，内在自我评估最弱）；失败模式（自我确认循环、模型崩溃、多样性崩溃）源于违反该层次；研究方向设定是保持人在回路的关键瓶颈；治理级测量是研究最少的领域。

Conclusion: 有界的自我完善已具备工业实践条件，而开放式的递归自我改进在基础需求、崩溃动力学和计算约束上仍受限制；自我评估替代人类判断的主张需要更严格的验证。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly participate in their own improvement: revising their outputs, adapting their own harnesses during deployment, training on data they generate, and, increasingly, conducting AI research itself. This literature is described under a vocabulary ("self-refine," "self-reward," "self-play," "self-evolve") that conflates fundamentally different ambitions. We survey 1,250 arXiv papers (2024-2026) along two axes: what the system improves -- its behavior in deployment, its policy through training, its evaluator, or the research process itself -- and the degree of loop closure (human-in-the-loop to fully closed). The taxonomy separates bounded self-refinement -- convergent, evaluable, and already industrial practice -- from open-ended recursive self-improvement (RSI), which remains bounded by grounding requirements, collapse dynamics, and compute constraints on every measured axis. Its distinctive feature is a dedicated category for self-evaluation: every improvement loop is a claim that some signal can substitute for human judgment. We survey the evaluator design space -- judges, process reward models, verifiers, rubrics, meta-evaluation -- order the signals into a verification hierarchy from formal verifiers (strongest) to intrinsic self-assessment (weakest), and observe that demonstrated self-improvement strength tracks this hierarchy, that its failure modes (self-confirming loops, model collapse, diversity collapse) follow from its violations, and that the "research direction-setting" bottleneck keeping humans in the loop sits at the top of that hierarchy. We connect the technical literature to the theory of RSI limits and to the safety and governance questions raised by frontier-lab accounts of closing the loop, and identify governance-grade measurement of self-improvement as the field's most underpopulated niche.

</details>


### [46] [SkillCenter: A Large-Scale Source-Grounded Skill Library for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07676)
*Tianming Sha,Yue Zhao,Lichao Sun,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillCenter 是当前最大的开放智能体技能库，包含 216,938 个结构化技能，覆盖 24 个领域。通过 SkillGate 过滤管道，从同行评审期刊、ArXiv 等 24,000+ 技术来源获取 114,565 个源可追溯技能，并结合 GitHub 和 ClawHub 的 102,373 个社区技能。所有技能均以离线可搜索的 SQLite FTS5 包形式发布，并保证每条声明可追溯至原始来源的精确引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 AI 智能体在执行复杂任务时缺乏基于操作经验的接地知识，导致输出虽然可执行，但在正确性、安全性和可维护性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出端到端框架：多源采集、基于 LLM 的质量门控（SkillGate）、模板驱动生成、迭代源接地（source-grounding）以及质量受控发布。源接地通过精确引用原文引文实现可追溯性保证。

Result: 构建了包含 216,938 个结构化技能的 SkillCenter 技能库，其中 114,565 个来自经过 SkillGate 过滤的学术及技术来源，102,373 个来自社区贡献。

Conclusion: SkillCenter 通过大规模、源可追溯的技能库，为 AI 智能体提供了可靠的操作知识，提升了输出的正确性、安全性和可维护性。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents can execute complex tasks with limited human review, yet they often lack the grounded operational knowledge to make their outputs not just executable but correct, secure, and maintainable. We introduce SkillCenter, to our knowledge the largest open skill library for agents by total count: 216,938 structured skills across 24 domain bundles. A SkillGate-filtered pipeline contributes 114,565 source-grounded skills from peer-reviewed journals, ArXiv, and over 24,000 technical sources, integrated with 102,373 community skills from GitHub and the ClawHub marketplace. We present the end-to-end framework that builds the pipeline subset: multi-source acquisition, an LLM-based quality gate (SkillGate), template-driven generation, iterative source-grounding, and quality-controlled publishing. Source grounding is a traceability guarantee: each retained claim maps to an exact quotation in its source. All skills ship as offline-searchable SQLite FTS5 bundles.

</details>


### [47] [Institutional Red-Teaming: Deployment Rules, Not Just Models, Causally Shape Multi-Agent AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07695)
*Yujiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多智能体AI部署规则评估的"机构红队测试"方法，通过固定智能体、目标和任务状态，仅改变单一规则并归因集体行为变化。基于IABench-CA基准（228个场景、5种规则、7个模型种群，共33,924局游戏）的实证发现：部署规则能因果性地改变集体安全性（仅改变后果规则可使平均死亡率移动22-58个百分点）；不存在安全的默认规则，且"定向危害"具有普遍性——最安全/最不安全的规则及发病率效应方向均因种群而异，但回归性身份针对从未成为任何场景下的最安全选择；身份显著性是其作用机制（匿名化消融实验显示，仅命名损失承担者即可将针对性消除从22%提升至81%）；该方法可作为安全验证工作流，为每个部署场景和种群认证临时规则区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全评估多聚焦单智能体行为，缺乏对多智能体系统中部署规则（如后果分配规则）如何影响集体安全行为的系统性评估方法论。

Method: 提出机构红队测试（institutional red-teaming），通过固定智能体、目标和任务状态，仅改变一个规则变量来归因集体行为变化。构建IABench-CA基准，涵盖228个场景、5种规范规则、7个模型种群，共33,924局游戏，并配备规范性合作参考和自动标注推理轨迹。

Result: (1) 部署规则因果性改变集体安全性，仅改变后果规则可使平均死亡率移动22-58个百分点；(2) 不存在安全默认规则，回归性身份针对在30-87%游戏中消除最弱资源智能体，且对全部7个种群而言相对于合作参考是选择不安全的；(3) 身份显著性驱动针对行为，匿名化消融使针对性消除从22%升至81%，重复博弈下匿名化仅推迟针对。

Conclusion: 提出机构红队测试可作为安全验证工作流，为每个部署场景和种群认证临时规则区域，明确残余风险和监控义务。

Abstract: We introduce institutional red-teaming, an evaluation methodology for testing deployment rules in multi-agent AI: hold the agents, objectives, and task state fixed, vary only one rule, and attribute the resulting change in collective behavior to that rule. We instantiate the methodology in IABench-CA, a consequence-allocation benchmark spanning 228 contexts, five canonical rules, and seven model populations (33,924 games), with a normative cooperative reference and auto-labelled reasoning traces. Three findings emerge. (1) Deployment rules causally alter collective safety: changing only the consequence rule moves mean fatality by 22 to 58 percentage points within every population. (2) There is no safe default, but the targeting hazard is universal: the safest rule, the least-safe rule, and even the direction of the incidence effect vary across populations, yet regressive identity-targeting is never decisively safest in any context for any population, eliminates the least-resourced agent in 30-87% of games everywhere, and is selection-unsafe relative to the cooperative reference for all seven populations. (3) Identity salience is the mechanism: a one-shot anonymization ablation on the most exploitation-prone population (gpt-5.1) shows that merely naming the loss bearer in the rule text drives targeted elimination from 22% to 81% at identical payoffs; under repeated play, anonymization only delays the targeting, as agents re-infer the hidden rule from observed eliminations. We package the methodology as a safety-case workflow that certifies a provisional rule region $Φ(c,P)$ per deployment context and population, with explicit residual risks and monitoring obligations.

</details>

### [48] [TriRoute: Unified Learned Routing for Joint Adaptive Attention, Experts, and KV-Cache Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06601)
*Andrii Balashov,Olena Ponomarova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriRoute提出一个轻量级控制器，联合优化注意力模式、FFN专家选择和KV缓存位宽三个轴，在匹配推理FLOPs和内存下Pareto优于独立MoD+MoE+KV量化组合，并能更好地保持对稀有实体、代码等尾部样例的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件计算技术（MoE、MoD、KV缓存量化）各自孤立地作用于单一轴，忽略了注意力分辨率、专家选择和缓存位宽三个决策之间存在强耦合关系。例如，需要全注意力的token可能也需要高精度缓存，但这些关联未被联合建模。

Method: 提出TriRoute，一个轻量级共享控制器，为每层每个token联合输出三项策略：(i)注意力模式（跳过/局部/全注意力），(ii)稀疏FFN专家集（含空专家恢复MoD），(iii)KV缓存位宽。通过异构松弛（Gumbel-Softmax+直通估计处理分类决策，负载均衡top-k门控处理专家选择）实现端到端训练，并采用拉格朗日预算约束将计算和内存成本转化为可控旋钮。针对联合训练中出现的跨轴路由坍塌级联，引入逐轴归一化和耦合感知均衡损失。

Result: 在160M到1.3B参数的decoder-only模型上，TriRoute在匹配推理FLOPs和内存下Pareto优于独立MoD+MoE+KV量化组合。能更好地保持对稀有实体、代码和算术等尾部样例的鲁棒性。后验分析显示控制器有可解释的结构：将全注意力和高精度缓存分配给句首位置、稀有子词和命名实体，而功能词则使用廉价路由。

Conclusion: 注意力分辨率、专家选择和缓存位宽三个决策应联合优化，TriRoute证明了跨轴联合路由的有效性，既提升了效率，又保持了尾部鲁棒性。

Abstract: Conditional computation can decouple language model quality from per-token inference cost, yet leading techniques act on a single axis in isolation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) sparsifies the FFN, Mixture-of-Depths (MoD) skips whole transformer blocks, and KV-cache quantization compresses attention memory. We argue these three decisions (attention resolution, expert selection, and cache bit-width) are strongly coupled and should be made jointly: a token rare enough to warrant full attention may also need high-precision caching regardless of which expert processes it. We introduce TriRoute, a single lightweight controller shared across all three axes that, for every token at every layer, emits a coordinated policy: (i) an attention mode (skip/local/full), (ii) a sparse set of FFN experts (with a null expert recovering MoD), and (iii) a KV-cache bit-width. The controller trains end-to-end via a heterogeneous relaxation (Gumbel-Softmax with straight-through estimation for categorical decisions and load-balanced top-k gating for experts) under a Lagrangian budget constraint that turns the average compute and memory cost into a controllable knob. We identify a cross-axis routing-collapse cascade in naive joint training, where collapse on one axis propagates to the others, and address it with per-axis normalization and a coupling-aware balancing loss. On decoder-only models from 160M to 1.3B parameters at compute-optimal token counts, TriRoute Pareto-dominates the best independent MoD+MoE+KV-quantization combination at matched inference FLOPs and memory, while better preserving tail-case robustness on rare entities, code, and arithmetic that pure perplexity optimization erodes. Post-hoc analysis reveals interpretable structure: the controller allocates full attention and high-precision cache to sentence-initial positions, rare subwords, and named entities, while cheaply routing function words.

</details>


### [49] [A Quiet Failure in Calibrated Virtual Screening: Marginal Conformal Prediction Under-Covers the Minority Class, and a Class-Conditional Fix Recovers It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06605)
*Muhammadjon Tursunbadalov,Mustafojon Tursunbadalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了标准共形预测在药物发现中不平衡数据集上的严重缺陷：虽然全局覆盖率达到90%，但少数类覆盖率可低至4.2%。该问题源于保守恒恒等式，可用Mondrian共形预测修复，并提出了诊断指标和实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测被广泛应用于药物发现以量化模型可靠性，但其标准（边际）形式在类别不平衡数据上可能存在严重问题，作者希望揭示这一隐患并给出解决方案。

Method: 在四个药物发现数据集（血脑屏障渗透、临床试验毒性等）上，使用随机森林、图神经网络和化学语言模型三种架构，对比标准共形预测与类别条件（Mondrian）共形预测的表现。通过守恒恒等式理论分析少数类覆盖率的偏差。

Result: 标准共形预测全局覆盖率达90%，但少数类覆盖率在血脑屏障数据上降至64.8%，在临床毒性数据上仅4.2%。该现象在不同模型架构中一致出现（p<0.001），且与基线校准相关而非模型架构。守恒恒等式精确预测了覆盖差距。Mondrian共形预测在所有数据集上恢复了少数类覆盖率达到目标水平。

Conclusion: 标准共形预测在不平衡数据上会系统性牺牲少数类可靠性，而这一缺陷被良好的全局指标所掩盖。采用类别条件共形预测可有效修复，并提供了一数字诊断指标和实际成本模型指导应用。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is being adopted in drug discovery to put an honest number on model reliability: pick an error rate alpha, and the method returns prediction sets containing the true label with probability at least 1 - alpha. We show this guarantee can be dangerous on imbalanced datasets. Across four datasets, standard (marginal) conformal prediction hits its global 90% coverage target while leaving the minority class badly exposed: realized minority coverage falls to 64.8% on blood-brain-barrier penetration and to 4.2% on clinical-trial toxicity, where the rare class is nearly abandoned. The failure is not tied to one model: a random forest, a graph network, and a frozen chemical language model all reproduce it (p < 0.001 in every case), with severity tracking baseline calibration on rare labels rather than architecture. A conservation identity explains the effect: the minority's shortfall equals the majority's surplus amplified by the imbalance ratio, predicting the measured gap to within one point and ordering severity across datasets. The failure survives realistic scaffold splits and a second conformal score, while aggregate accuracy and overall coverage stay reassuringly high, which is exactly why it is easy to miss. Class-conditional (Mondrian) conformal prediction closes the gap on every dataset, restoring minority coverage to target for a modest increase in prediction-set size. We localize the failures to generic molecular scaffolds - plain benzene and pyridine cores occurring in both classes - propose a one-number diagnostic, and show with a cost model that abstaining on affected compounds flips a screening campaign from net-negative to net-positive utility. Our contribution is demonstrating on real chemistry how severe and invisible this known conformal-theory gap becomes under imbalance, and laying out a practical protocol restoring per-class reliability.

</details>


### [50] [NEST: Tackling Dataset-Level Distribution Shifts via Regime-Oriented Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06607)
*Lanhao Li,Bingshu Xie,Lijun Sun,Xin Xue,Haoyi Zhou,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEST提出了一种两阶段密集MoE架构，通过无监督聚类将数据集划分为不同运行状态，利用面向状态的路由器机制生成专家权重，使各专家作为专用核捕获状态特定的动态模式，在异构网络流量和物理现象等基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注局部时间偏移，未能显式建模数据集由不同运行状态组成的全局结构挑战，导致在复杂系统中的长期预测准确度受限。

Method: 提出NEST框架：1) 在矩-熵空间通过无监督聚类将数据集划分为不同运行状态；2) 设计面向状态的路由器机制，基于时序内容生成初始专家权重，并通过几何调制向状态质心精炼；3) 各专家作为专用核，通过演化独特的变量注意力模式捕获状态特定动态。

Result: 在异构网络流量和物理现象等多个多样基准上，NEST一致达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: NEST通过显式建模和重组数据的演化结构解决了长期预测中的分布偏移问题，验证了基于状态分解的MoE架构的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate long-term forecasting in complex systems is frequently compromised by dataset-level distribution shifts, where diverse underlying behavioral modes and evolving system states drive the dynamic multivariate time-series. While existing methods predominantly focus on local temporal shifts, they fail to explicitly model the global structural challenge where datasets are composites of distinct operational regimes. In this paper, we propose NEST, a specialized framework designed to model and recompose these evolving structures through a two-phase dense MoE architecture. NEST first facilitates structural specialization by partitioning the dataset into distinct operational regimes through unsupervised clustering in a principled moment-entropy space. We introduce a regime-oriented router mechanism that generates initial expert weights based on temporal content, subsequently refined through geometric modulation to regime centroids. Crucially, rather than acting as monolithic predictors, individual experts function as specialized kernels that capture regime-specific dynamics by evolving unique variate-attention patterns. Extensive evaluations on diverse benchmarks, including heterogeneous network traffic and physical phenomena, demonstrate that NEST consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/Aaralshin/NEST

</details>


### [51] [D2PO: Optimizing Diffusion Samplers via Dynamic Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06609)
*Jinkyu Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: D2PO将扩散采样器优化重新表述为基于偏好的对齐问题，通过能量模型将DPO应用于扩散采样策略，并引入动态偏好机制实现自改进，在低NFE下优于传统回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学生-教师回归框架中，低NFE学生采样器模仿高NFE教师时，牺牲高频纹理保真度而保留粗粒度全局结构，导致采样器与感知质量不对齐。

Method: 将采样策略建模为能量基模型(EBM)，将偏好比较转化为可处理的能量差；从预训练分数网络推导出新的能量公式，实现扰动空间中的偏好评估；引入动态偏好机制，偏好样本随策略学习逐步改进。

Result: D2PO更忠实地对齐扩散采样器与感知质量，释放高质量教师的全部潜力，在低NFE约束下持续优于传统回归调度器。

Conclusion: 偏好引导的自改进机制替代刚性静态教师监督，提供渐进增强的对齐信号，有效解决了回归框架中纹理细节丢失问题。

Abstract: We propose D2PO (Dynamic Direct Preference Optimization), a principled framework for optimizing diffusion sampling policies with respect to timestep schedules and classifier-free guidance (CFG) weights. Our work is motivated by a fundamental limitation of existing student-teacher regression frameworks; low-NFE student samplers are trained to mimic high-NFEteachers, often sacrificing high-frequency texture fidelity while preserving coarse global structures, thereby misaligning the sampler with perceptual quality. D2PO addresses this challenge by reformulating sampler optimization as a preference-based alignment problem, leveraging the Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) framework. To make DPO applicable to diffusion samplers, we model the sampling policy as an energy-based model (EBM), transforming preference comparisons into tractable energy differences. We further introduce a novel energy formulation derived directly from the pretrained score network, enabling preference evaluation in perturbed spaces that jointly capture structural consistency and fine-grained details. Moreover, we introduce dynamic preferences, where the preferred samples used for alignment progressively improve as the sampling policies are learned. This self-improving mechanism replaces rigid static teacher supervision with an iterative, preference-guided refinement process, providing progressively stronger alignment signals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that D2PO aligns diffusion samplers with perceptual quality more faithfully, unlocking the full potential of high-quality teachers and consistently outperforming conventional regression-based schedulers under low-NFE constraints.

</details>


### [52] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Reliability Based Bi-Objective Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06610)
*Sounaq Das,Tanmay Sen,Raghu Nandan Sengupta,Aditya Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度强化学习的多目标可靠性投资组合优化框架（MORP-DRL），该框架联合优化期望收益与下行风险，结合GARCH(1,1)、极值理论和t-copula建模市场不确定性，使用PPO策略在考虑交易成本和组合约束条件下进行投资组合决策，在十只全球股指的多个市场时期中验证了其风险收益表现和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可靠性的投资组合优化方法主要依赖静态优化框架，无法捕捉序列决策、尾部风险及交易成本等市场摩擦因素，亟需一种能够处理多目标优化和动态环境的方法。

Method: 构建MORP-DRL框架，联合使用方差、CVaR和EVaR三种互补风险度量；采用GARCH(1,1)、极值理论和t-copula对资产回报的肥尾特性和相依结构建模；利用准蒙特卡洛模拟生成现实场景；基于PPO算法在交易成本和投资组合边界等约束下进行策略优化，并以NSGA-II作为基线对比。

Result: 在COVID前、COVID期间和COVID后三种市场机制下的十只全球股指实验中，MORP-DRL实现了竞争性的风险收益表现，在市场压力期间有效降低了下行风险，并展现了在高维投资组合场景中的可扩展性。

Conclusion: MORP-DRL通过深度强化学习成功克服了传统静态优化方法的局限，能够有效处理多目标不确定性下的序列决策问题，为投资组合优化提供了一个实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Portfolio optimization under uncertainty is inherently a multi-objective decision problem involving complex interactions among return, risk, market dynamics, and practical investment constraints. Existing reliability based portfolio optimization approaches primarily rely on static optimization frameworks and often fail to capture sequential decision making, tail risk, and market frictions such as transaction costs. To address these limitations, we propose a deep reinforcement learning framework for multi-objective reliability based portfolio optimization (MORP-DRL). The proposed framework jointly optimizes expected return and downside risk using three complementary risk measures: variance, Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR), and Entropic Value-at-Risk (EVaR). To model uncertainty and heavy-tailed market behavior, asset returns are represented using GARCH(1,1), Extreme Value Theory, and a t-copula dependence structure, while realistic scenarios are generated through quasi-Monte Carlo simulation. A Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) based strategy is developed under practical constraints including transaction costs and portfolio bounds, and is benchmarked against NSGA-II. Experiments on ten global equity indices across pre-COVID, COVID, and post-COVID market regimes demonstrate that MORP-DRL achieves competitive risk-return performance, reduced downside risk during periods of market stress, and scalability to high-dimensional portfolio settings.

</details>


### [53] [STAGformer: A Spatio-temporal Agent Graph Transformer for Micro Mobility Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06614)
*Ye Zihao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAGformer模型，通过时空代理图注意力机制以线性复杂度实现全局建模，在纽约和芝加哥共享单车数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统中车站级需求预测因复杂的时空依赖和大规模城市网络而具有挑战性，现有方法难以高效地同时捕捉局部和全局时空依赖关系。

Method: 提出STAGformer模型，包含四模块：时空编码器（融合动态节点特征与外部上下文因素）、图传播模块（空间邻域聚合）、时序卷积模块（局部模式提取）和代理注意力模块（全局依赖建模）。核心创新在于两阶段代理注意力机制，利用少量可学习的空间和时间代理令牌先聚合全局信息再广播回各车站和时间步，将标准自注意力的二次复杂度降至线性O(NT)。

Result: 在纽约Citi-Bike和芝加哥Divvy-Bike两个真实数据集上，STAGformer在多个预测时域下均优于现有最优基线方法，在RMSE和MAE上取得显著改进。消融实验验证了各组件的贡献，代理注意力机制对全局时空依赖建模至关重要。

Conclusion: STAGformer通过线性复杂度的代理注意力机制有效解决了共享单车需求预测中的全局时空依赖建模问题，在真实数据集上验证了其有效性，为大规模时空预测任务提供了新的高效方案。

Abstract: Accurate station-level demand forecasting is essential for the efficient operation of bike-sharing systems, yet it remains challenging due to complex spatio-temporal dependencies and the large scale of urban networks. This paper presents STAGformer, a Spatio-Temporal Agent Graph Transformer that achieves efficient global modeling with linear computational complexity. The model introduces a two-step agent attention mechanism, where a small set of learnable spatial and temporal agent tokens first aggregate global information and then broadcast it back to individual stations and time steps, effectively capturing long-range interactions while reducing the quadratic cost of standard self-attention to O(NT). STAGformer integrates four core modules: a spatio-temporal encoder that fuses dynamic node features with external contextual factors (weather, time, points of interest), a graph propagation module for spatial neighbor aggregation, a temporal convolution module for local pattern extraction, and the agent attention module for global dependency modeling. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets -- NYC Citi-Bike and Chicago Divvy-Bike -- demonstrate that STAGformer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple prediction horizons, achieving significant improvements in both RMSE and MAE. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each component, with the agent attention mechanism proving critical for modeling global spatio-temporal dependencies.

</details>


### [54] [WHERE to Generate Matters: Budget-Aware Synthetic Augmentation for Label Skewed Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06616)
*Sangwoo Lee,Sunghwan Park,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedEAS通过熵自适应每个类别的生成预算策略，在联邦学习中高效缓解标签不平衡问题，相比完全类别平衡节省94.1%的计算成本，同时恢复大部分精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中标签偏移导致客户端漂移和全局精度下降；现有的合成数据增强方法虽然能缓解不平衡，但完全类别平衡需要巨大的计算开销。

Method: 提出FedEAS策略，为每个客户端基于其局部标签分布计算熵自适应的每类生成预算，该预算联合决定每个客户端生成多少样本以及样本分配到哪里；总生成预算由各客户端预算动态决定而非预先固定。

Result: FedEAS恢复了完全类别平衡的大部分精度增益，同时将生成预算减少了94.1%；在相同总生成预算下，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上相比均匀分配策略提升高达18.82%。

Conclusion: FedEAS通过自适应预算分配高效解决了联邦学习中的标签不平衡问题，显著降低了计算成本，同时保持高精度。

Abstract: Label skew in federated learning (FL) causes client drift and degrades global accuracy. Synthetic data augmentation can reduce this imbalance; however, full class balancing requires substantial computation cost. We propose FedEAS, a policy that assigns each client an entropy-adaptive per-class generation budget computed from its local label distribution. The budget jointly decides \emph{how much} each client generates and \emph{WHERE} the samples go. Accordingly, the total generation budget follows from the per-client budgets rather than being fixed in advance. FedEAS recovers most of the accuracy gain of full class balancing while reducing the generation budget by 94.1\%. At the same total generation budget, it outperforms Uniform allocation by up to 18.82\% across CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [55] [From Jumps to Signatures: a Generative Method for Temporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06652)
*Niels Cariou-Kotlarek,Vasileios Lampos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了sigTPP，首个基于程签名的时序点过程生成模型，通过引入跳间嵌入（interarrival embedding）将跳跃路径连续化，实现了序列级路径损失训练，并推导了三种分布差异度量用于评估变长事件序列生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗路程签名（rough path signatures）是连续路径的通用特征映射，并通过期望签名刻画路径分布，但这些保证不直接适用于时序点过程（TPPs）的右连左极（cadlag）路径，限制了签名方法在事件序列上的应用。同时，神经TPP模型（包括最近的生成方法）优化的是每个事件的逐点目标，缺乏全局序列级损失；而变长事件序列的评估也缺少分布差异度量。

Method: 提出跳间嵌入（interarrival embedding），一种从跳跃路径到有界变差连续路径的稳定、单射的提升映射，将签名方法扩展到离散事件序列。基于此构建sigTPP，首个基于签名的TPP生成模型，使用完整轨迹上的路径级损失进行训练。进一步分析计数路径空间，推导三种分布差异度量。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，sigTPP基于八项互补指标获得最佳平均排名，在64%的数据集-指标对中优于或在一个标准误差内与最强基线持平，根据相对得分，每个基线平均至少提升19%。

Conclusion: 跳间嵌入有效将签名方法扩展到离散事件序列，sigTPP模型在生成质量上显著优于现有方法，所提出的分布差异度量为评估变长序列生成模型提供了数学上合理的工具。

Abstract: Rough path signatures are a universal feature map for continuous paths and, via the expected signature, characterise path distributions. These guarantees do not directly extend to cadlag paths of Temporal Point Processes (TPPs), limiting the use of signature methods for event sequences. Furthermore, neural TPP models, including recent generative approaches, optimise per-event objectives with no global sequence-level loss, while evaluation of variable-length event sequences lacks distributional discrepancy measures. This paper proposes a common pathwise framework for addressing these limitations. We introduce the interarrival embedding, a stable, injective lift from jump paths to continuous paths of bounded variation, extending signature methods to discrete event sequences. Our theoretical contributions give rise to sigTPP, the first signature-based generative model for TPPs, trained using a path-level loss on complete trajectories. We further analyse the space of counting paths and derive three distributional discrepancies, providing mathematically justified tools for evaluating generative TPP models. Across synthetic and real-world datasets, sigTPP achieves the best average rank based on eight complementary metrics, outperforms or is within a standard error of the strongest baseline in 64% of the dataset-metric pairs, and according to a relative score, improves against every baseline by at least 19% on average.

</details>


### [56] [Inertia-1: An Open Exploration of Wearable Motion Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06617)
*Zongzhe Xu,Aakarsh Anand,Sarah Jiang,Chuntung Zhuang,Zitao Shuai,Sriram Sankararaman,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Inertia-1，一个完全开放的穿戴式运动基础模型探索框架，基于超过1820万小时的全球加速度计数据，系统研究了数据选择（传感器模态、设备位置、采样率、窗口长度）、模型选择（架构、规模）和训练选择（预训练目标、数据规模）对下游任务的影响，并在15个数据集上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿戴式运动感知为人类行为和健康监测提供了连续可扩展的窗口，适合构建基础模型，但其预训练和扩展原理尚不明确。现有研究在固定设置和狭窄下游任务下研究孤立的设计选择，无法捕捉真实世界的感知多样性。

Method: 基于1820万+小时全球加速度计数据，构建可控框架系统研究穿戴式运动基础模型的完整生命周期，涵盖数据选择、模型选择和训练选择三大维度，并在15个数据集上评估。

Result: 在人类活动识别、冻结步态检测和疾病预测等15个数据集上揭示了构建泛化运动基础模型的关键发现，并取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: Inertia-1不仅为多种下游任务提供了最先进的方案，也作为一份全面、实用且开放的运动表示学习指南。

Abstract: Wearable motion sensing provides a continuous and scalable window into human behavior and health, making it a natural fit for foundation models, yet its pretraining and scaling principles remain poorly understood. Prior work studies isolated design choices, such as sensor placement or sampling frequency, often under fixed settings and narrow downstream tasks that fail to capture real-world sensing diversity. We introduce Inertia-1, a fully open exploration of wearable motion foundation models. Using massive corpora of accelerometer data from global sources spanning more than 18.2M hours, we build a controlled framework for studying the full lifecycle of wearable motion foundation models, covering data choices such as sensor modality, device placement, sampling rate, window length; model choices such as architectures and model size; and training choices such as pretraining objective and data scale. Extensive evaluations across 15 datasets spanning human activity recognition, freezing-of-gait detection, and disease prediction reveal intriguing findings for building motion foundation models that generalize across tasks and sensing conditions. Collectively, Inertia-1 not only presents state-of-the-art recipes for diverse downstream tasks, but also serves as a comprehensive, practical, and open cookbook for wearable motion representation learning.

</details>


### [57] [Best-Arm Identification with Generative Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06879)
*Tianyi Ma,Hanzhang Qin,Ruihao Zhu,Jierui Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PROBE 算法，在固定置信度 best-arm identification 中利用廉价代理分数（与奖励存在未知相关性 ρ）来降低样本复杂度，通过控制变量调整将问题转化为异方差识别问题，并使用普通最小二乘法保持残差方差的上界证书，实现与已知相关性情况相当的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际决策场景中，获取奖励观测成本高昂，但机器学习和大型语言模型可以提供廉价但相关的代理分数。现有方法未充分利用这种代理信息，且代理与奖励的相关性未知，需要在线学习。本文旨在设计一种能够利用代理分数来降低 costly reward pulls 数量的算法。

Method: 提出 PROBE (PRoxy OLS for Best-arm Exploration) 算法：1) 使用控制变量调整将问题转化为异方差 best-arm identification 问题；2) 采用 phase-elimination 架构；3) 通过普通最小二乘法 (OLS) 在线拟合代理与奖励的关系；4) 利用卡方分布维持残差方差的上界证书，确保无论未知相关性如何，证书始终有效。

Result: PROBE 是 δ-PAC 的，且达到已知相关性情况下的 oracle 样本复杂度，仅相差常数乘性因子和常数加性校准成本。该保证可扩展到 (ε,δ)-PAC 设置。数值实验（含合成数据和基于大型语言模型及表格代理的汽车贷款定价回放）证实样本节省量与奖励-代理相关性强度成正比。

Conclusion: PROBE 有效地利用廉价代理信息降低 costly best-arm identification 的样本复杂度，其理论保证与实际表现一致。当代理与奖励强相关时，样本节省显著，为数据驱动决策中利用预测模型和语言模型提供了理论支持和实用算法。

Abstract: Best-arm identification is a canonical model for data-driven decision-making, but in many applications each reward observation is costly. Motivated by the growing availability of cheap predictions from machine learning and large language models, we study fixed-confidence best-arm identification in which each costly reward pull is paired with a cheap but correlated proxy score. The marginal mean of the proxy can be estimated offline and is treated as known, whereas its correlation $ρ$ with the reward, which governs how much the proxy helps, is unknown and must be learned online in pair with real rewards. We show that a control-variate adjustment turns this model into a heteroscedastic identification problem whose oracle sample complexity improves by residual variance $1-ρ^2$. The central difficulty is that the correlation must be learned from the same costly samples that identification consumes online, and that a plug-in estimate of the residual variance is anti-conservative and can compromise correctness. We propose PROBE (PRoxy OLS for Best-arm Exploration), a phase-elimination algorithm that directly maintains an upper certificate on the residual variance with an ordinary least squares fit, whose exact chi-square law keeps the certificate valid regardless of the unknown correlation. We prove that PROBE is $δ$-PAC and attains the known-correlation oracle sample complexity up to a constant multiplicative factor and a constant additive calibration cost. The guarantee extends to the $(ε,δ)$-PAC setting under minimal changes to the algorithm. Numerical experiments on synthetic instances and on an auto-loan pricing replay with large language model and tabular proxies confirm that the sample savings of PROBE scale with the strength of the reward-proxy correlation, exactly as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [58] [Fingerprint, Not Blueprint: How Positional Schemes Set the Default Spectral Algebra of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06621)
*Li Hengyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力头中pre-softmax分数的双线性形式$score(i,j)=x_i^T M x_j$，其中$M = W_q^T W_k$通常为非对称非正规矩阵。在三个层面（静态、动态、因果）分析其复特征谱在previous-token和induction电路中的编码含义：静态下，RoPE模型呈现谱旋转性，而绝对位置和ALiBi模型呈现内容性；动态下，每个头都从随机矩阵零点起源，旋转特征随行为出现而非之前；因果上，无谱通道是必要的，但位置方案设定了注意力头解的默认谱代数，功能后形成的指纹而非硬约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索注意力头中pre-softmax分数的非对称矩阵M的复特征谱所编码的信息，理解不同位置编码方案（RoPE、绝对位置、ALiBi）下注意力头的计算机制差异。

Method: 在三个层次分析：静态层面对比七种预训练模型（三种位置方案）；动态层面追踪Pythia检查点中头的演化；因果层面通过约束训练（禁止谱通道）观察影响。

Result: RoPE模型的前一token头呈现谱旋转性（复特征值旋转对称），绝对位置和ALiBi模型呈现内容性（非旋转）；动态演化中旋转特征随功能出现；因果上无单个谱通道是必要的，但对称性约束会延迟学习（绝对位置模型延迟2.9倍）。

Conclusion: 位置编码方案设定了注意力头解的默认谱代数，特征谱是功能形成后的指纹（fingerprint）而非硬约束（hard constraint）。

Abstract: The pre-softmax score of an attention head is a bilinear form $score(i,j) = x_i^T M x_j$ in a learned operator $M = W_q^T W_k$. Because M is generally non-symmetric, hence non-normal, it has a complex eigenspectrum and non-orthogonal eigenvectors, the regime where non-Hermitian and random-matrix tools apply. We ask what this spectrum encodes, at three levels for previous-token and induction circuits. Statically, across seven pretrained models spanning three positional schemes, the strongest previous-token heads are spectrally rotational under RoPE and non-rotational, or content-like, where position enters outside QK (learned-absolute and ALiBi); the model-level separation is perfect at every top-k examined (exact permutation $p=0.029$), and zeroing the per-frequency RoPE phase $Im(M_t)$ eliminates induction on a pre-identified previous-token head in all three RoPE models. Dynamically, over public Pythia checkpoints every head originates at the random-matrix (Ginibre) null; the rotational signature emerges with the behavior, not before it, and the population-median suppression that yields the final profile follows circuit formation, so the profile is a consolidated fingerprint, not a precursor. Causally, and at toy scale, no spectral channel is necessary: constrained two-layer training reroutes around every ban with capability intact, albeit at a significant formation delay (four pre-registered contrasts, $q_BH <= 0.016$). The cost structure exposes each scheme's default: imposing symmetry slows learned-absolute models by a factor of 2.9, whereas a RoPE head with a fully symmetric static M still routes directionally via the phase channel, impossible under absolute positions. Within the settings examined, the positional scheme sets the default spectral algebra of an attention head's solution: a fingerprint sculpted after function, not a hard constraint upon it.

</details>

*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于磁Krylov子空间的有向图频谱位置编码方法，通过可学习的矩阵函数 $h_\theta(A_q)R$ 实现规范不变性，利用块Krylov子空间高效计算，理论上证明了 $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 步收敛，在有向随机块模型上优于方向盲PE，并在无向异质图上超越多项式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图的频谱位置编码面临两大障碍：磁拉普拉斯算子需 $O(n^3)$ 的厄米特特征分解，且复特征向量存在酉规范不确定性，现有工作需依赖基不变架构来处理。

Method: 提出可学习频谱PE形式 $h_\theta(A_q)R$，其中 $A_q$ 为归一化磁算子，$h_\theta$ 为可学习标量频谱响应，$R$ 为随机探针块。在厄米特块Krylov子空间中仅通过稀疏矩阵-向量乘积计算，证明了 $k = O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 步数在热-预解响应族上一致收敛，并给出覆盖数论证解释低维结构化族为何优于自由逐特征值权重。

Result: 在有向SBM上，方向盲PE仍为随机水平，而磁Krylov PE随深度增长收敛到精确特征分解预言机。相同探针以 $1/\sqrt{s}$ 蒙特卡洛误差产生规范不变的对特征，无向 $q=0$ 情形在异质基准上优于无PE和多项式基线。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过矩阵函数形式天然实现规范不变性，无需基不变架构，计算高效且理论保证充分，在有向和无向图上均表现优越。

Abstract: Spectral positional encodings (PEs) for \emph{directed} graphs face two obstacles: magnetic Laplacians require an $O(n^3)$ Hermitian eigendecomposition per potential, and their complex eigenvectors are defined only up to unitary gauge, which prior work handles with basis-invariant architectures. We propose learnable spectral PEs of the form $h_θ(A_q)\,R$, where $A_q$ is a normalized magnetic operator, $h_θ$ a learnable scalar spectral response, and $R$ a block of random probes. Because the PE is a \emph{matrix function} of the operator, it is gauge-invariant by construction. We compute it in a Hermitian block Krylov subspace from sparse matrix--vector products only, prove that $k = O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ block steps suffice uniformly over heat--resolvent response families, and give a covering-number argument for why low-dimensional structured families generalize where free per-eigenvalue weights overfit. On a directed SBM whose symmetrization is uninformative by construction, direction-blind PEs stay at chance while magnetic Krylov PEs converge to the exact-eigendecomposition oracle as the depth grows. The same probes yield gauge-invariant pairwise features with $1/\sqrt{s}$ Monte-Carlo error, and the undirected $q{=}0$ case improves heterophilous benchmarks over no-PE and polynomial baselines.

</details>


### [60] [LLM-Guided Task-Semantic Field Factorization for Industrial Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06623)
*Youcheng Zong,Runda Jia,Mingxuan Ren,Dakuo He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSF框架，利用LLM从工艺文档中构建任务语义场，将变量语义信息融入时间序列预测过程，在几乎不增加在线计算开销的前提下，在多个工业软测量与预测任务上平均降低MAE 6.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程工业中标签数据稀缺、运行工况频繁变化，现有时间序列模型将输入视为匿名数值列，未利用工艺文档中变量名称、单位、物理含义及过程角色等语义信息，且已有文本增强方法未在推理时将输入变量与预测目标间的语义逻辑关系引入模型。

Method: TSF框架：离线阶段利用LLM从任务协议和变量文档构建任务语义场；在线训练和推理时，当前数值窗口激活对应的变量语义信息，使语义参与每次预测，支持不同预测目标和工况变化的适应。

Result: 在多个复杂工业预测与软测量任务上，平均MAE降低6.4%，最大降幅达25.5%；仅增加约1.8–3.0k参数，在线推理额外开销小于0.008 ms/step。

Conclusion: TSF能够将现有工艺文档转化为可量化的预测性能提升，跨骨干网络和语义生成器均有效，且部署轻量。

Abstract: Process industries rely on time-series forecasting and soft sensing to estimate quality variables that are hard to measure online. Labeled data are scarce, operating regimes change frequently, and retraining models or rebuilding alignment pipelines for each scenario is costly. Such settings often provide variable tables and process documents that record variable names, units, physical meanings, and process roles. However, standard time-series backbones usually treat inputs as anonymous numerical columns. Existing text-enhanced methods also rarely make the semantic-logical relations between input variables and the prediction target available to the model within each numerical window. To address this problem, this article proposes Task-Semantic Field Factorization (TSF), a large language model (LLM)-guided framework. TSF builds a task-semantic field from task protocols and variable documents before training and uses the LLM only for offline semantic construction. Online training and inference remain with conventional time-series backbones. During training and inference, the current numerical window activates variable semantics, so semantic information participates in each prediction and supports adaptation to different prediction targets and operating shifts. On multiple complex industrial forecasting and soft-sensing tasks, TSF reduces MAE by 6.4\% on average in improved settings, with the largest reduction reaching 25.5\%. It adds only about 1.8--3.0k parameters, with less than 0.008 ms/step of additional online inference overhead. These results show that TSF turns existing process documents into measurable forecasting gains across backbones and semantic generators while remaining lightweight for deployment.

</details>


### [61] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Learning Autoregressive Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07423)
*Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在可实现的PAC学习框架下，完整自回归思维链迹的精确迹学习样本复杂度由局部下一词类的标准多类率上界决定，该率由Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维度控制。即使一个错误动作导致整个迹不正确，对于每个停止规则和每个逐点停驻局部类，样本复杂度为O((DSdim(H)+log(1/δ))/ε)，与展开长度无关。DSdim(H)的依赖是悲观最优的。证明引入了奇偶维度，一种基于偶伪立方体的展开稳定DS维度细化，并通过有限限制上的低坐标跨越定理控制一包含密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自回归思维链推理在可学习性理论中的样本复杂度特性，特别是当迹中任何单步错误都会导致整体失败时的精确迹学习难度。

Method: 引入奇偶维度（parity dimension）作为DS维度的展开稳定细化；利用偶伪立方体（even pseudo-cubes）和有限限制上的低坐标跨越定理来控制一包含密度；证明奇偶维度在自回归展开下不会增长，而DS维度可能增长。

Result: 精确迹学习的样本复杂度为O((DSdim(H)+log(1/δ))/ε)，与迹长度无关；DSdim(H)的依赖是最优的；奇偶维度是展开稳定的，而DS维度不具有此性质。

Conclusion: 自回归思维链的精确迹学习在PAC框架下具有与局部下一词类相同的样本复杂度率，不会因迹长度增加而恶化，但需要奇偶维度这一新工具来绕过DS维度在展开下可能增长的问题。

Abstract: We prove that, in the realizable PAC setting, the sample complexity of exact-trace learning for full autoregressive Chain-of-Thought traces is upper bounded by the standard multiclass rate of the local next-token class, where this rate is governed by the Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz dimension. Under exact-trace loss, one wrong action makes the whole trace incorrect; nevertheless, for every stopping rule $\mathtt{halt}$ and every pointwise $\mathtt{halt}$-halting local class $\mathrm{H}$, $n_{\mathrm{PAC}}^{\varepsilon,δ}(\operatorname{Roll}_{\mathtt{halt}}(\mathrm{H}))=O((\operatorname{DSdim}(\mathrm{H})+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon)$, with no dependence on rollout length. The dependence on $\operatorname{DSdim}(\mathrm{H})$ is worst-case optimal, since one-step stopping recovers ordinary multiclass learning of $\mathrm{H}$. The proof introduces parity dimension, a rollout-stable refinement of DS dimension based on even pseudo-cubes. It controls one-inclusion density via a low-coordinate spanning theorem on finite restrictions and, unlike DS dimension itself, does not increase under autoregressive rollout. We also show why this detour is necessary: DS dimension can increase under rollout.

</details>


### [62] [Open-Ended Scenario Reasoning for Specialist Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06625)
*Youcheng Zong,Runda Jia,Ranmeng Lin,Mingxuan Ren,Dakuo He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROAM框架，利用LLM的世界知识和推理能力，在不重新训练的情况下，通过低维语义可解释的潜在空间对冻结的工业专业模型进行自适应校正，在矿物浓缩和青霉素发酵数据集上MAE降低20%以上，仅需839个额外参数且每步开销低于0.02ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程中累积的专业模型因传感器漂移、原料变化和工况切换在新场景下性能退化，收集新标签数据重新训练成本高，而现有自适应方法需要大量标注数据修改模型参数，部署后难以快速响应。同时直接使用LLM作为预测器存在幻觉和不可控输出风险，且无法融入非结构化现场场景知识。

Method: ROAM框架将LLM生成的场景判断与在线观测融合到统一的概率框架中，所有校正约束在低维、语义可解释的潜在空间内执行。引入风险约束机制，在LLM证据不可靠或场景突变时抑制校正，退回到原始冻结模型。

Result: 在矿物浓缩过程和公共IndPenSim青霉素发酵数据集上，ROAM在隐藏漂移等主要偏移场景下MAE降低超过20%，仅需839个额外参数，每步开销低于0.02ms。

Conclusion: LLM的推理能力可以转化为已部署工业模型的保守自适应信号，实现在线快速响应而无需重新训练。

Abstract: Process industries have accumulated validated specialist models, yet sensor drift, feedstock variation, and regime switching cause these models to degrade systematically in new scenarios. Collecting new labeled data and retraining is costly, while continuing with the original model incurs persistent bias. Existing adaptation methods require modifying model parameters with sufficient labeled data, making rapid response on deployed systems difficult. Using LLMs as direct predictors risks hallucinations and uncontrollable outputs. Such predictors also cannot incorporate unstructured scenario knowledge from the field. To address these limitations, this article proposes Reasoning-Driven Open Adaptation for Specialist Models (ROAM), a framework that uses LLM world knowledge and reasoning to adapt frozen specialist models to unseen scenarios without retraining. ROAM confines all corrections to a low-dimensional, semantically interpretable latent space. LLM-generated scenario judgments and online observations are fused under a unified probabilistic framework. A risk-constrained mechanism suppresses corrections under unreliable LLM evidence or abrupt scenario shifts and falls back to the original frozen model when evidence is insufficient. Experiments on a mineral thickening process and the public IndPenSim penicillin fermentation dataset show that ROAM reduces MAE by over 20\% in major shift settings such as hidden shifts with only 839 additional parameters and under 0.02\,ms per-step overhead. These results indicate that LLM reasoning can be turned into a conservative adaptation signal for industrial models already in service.

</details>


### [63] [Fast Rates for Semi-Supervised Learning via Data-Augmentation Graph Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07513)
*Adam M. Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为自监督学习（SSL）的标签效率提供了理论解释：数据增强诱导的相似性图上的图拉普拉斯正则化学习可达到监督学习更快的标签收敛率 O(1/n_L)，并显式量化了增强质量对误差的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在实际中能用少量标签匹配监督学习的精度，但这种标签效率缺乏理论解释。

Method: 将数据增强诱导的相似图上的下游学习建模为图拉普拉斯正则化学习；将Johnson & Zhang的leave-one-out稳定性分析框架迁移到增强图上；使用精简损失函数（去掉投影头、负样本、正交性等开销）。

Result: 证明了一个快速的传递学习速率 O(1/n_L)，而监督学习为 O(1/√n_L)；误差上界为 C/n_L + R_DA(y)，其中R_DA(y)是跨越标签边界的数据增强的图割质量；在无限数据极限下可恢复top-K理想特征（增强核的特征空间）。

Conclusion: 该理论解释了观测到的准确率与标签数量的关系曲线，而不仅仅是给泛化间隙一个上界；好的数据增强只需少量标签即可达到高精度。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning matches supervised accuracy from a fraction of the labels, but the labeled-sample efficiency behind this has lacked a theoretical explanation. We provide one. Data augmentation induces a similarity graph on the unlabeled data, so downstream learning on that graph is graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. We prove a fast transductive rate, $O(1/n_L)$ in the number of labels, in place of the supervised $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$, by carrying the leave-one-out stability apparatus of Johnson and Zhang (JMLR 2007) over to the augmentation graph, and without the unrealistic assumptions of limit-based analyses (exact kernel, generalizing features). The bound makes augmentation quality explicit: the expected error is at most $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$, where the data-augmentation alignment error $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ is the graph-cut mass of augmentations that cross a label boundary, so good augmentations let few labels suffice. The analysis uses a streamlined loss that drops the projector, negative-sample, and orthogonality overhead of standard objectives yet still recovers the top-$K$ ideal features in the infinite-data limit, the augmentation-kernel eigenspace studied by Zhai et al. The result explains the observed accuracy-versus-label-count curve rather than only bounding a generalization gap.

</details>


### [64] [Reward Valuation in Vision Language Models: Causal Mechanisms Underlying Anhedonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06626)
*Melika Honarmand,Samin Mahdipour Aghabagher,Martin Schrimpf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型中存在与人类奖赏评估功能平行的神经回路，通过扰动模型中的"伏隔核选择性单元"可诱导出类似快感缺失的行为表现（转向低努力-低奖赏选项），且该缺陷特异于奖赏估值而非任务能力损失，与临床快感缺失量表（DARS、MAP-SR）表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉语言模型（VLM）是否在奖赏评估这一认知维度上与人类认知对齐，并利用神经科学中关于快感缺失（anhedonia）与伏隔核（NAc）及多巴胺奖赏系统失调的因果关联为框架，建立机制性测试方法。

Method: 从神经科学临床测试（评估抑郁症快感缺失与动机缺陷）出发，在VLM中功能性地识别出奖赏预期单元（reward-anticipatory units），通过靶向扰动（targeted perturbations）这些NAc选择性单元，观察模型在努力-奖赏决策任务中的行为变化。

Result: 扰动NAc选择性单元后，模型表现出类似人类快感缺失的行为：偏向低努力-低奖赏选项；当移除奖赏选项时，模型基线表现未受影响，表明缺陷特异于奖赏估值而非任务能力；该缺陷与临床快感缺失量表（DARS、MAP-SR）评分一致。

Conclusion: AI模型中存在与人类平行的奖赏估值神经回路，受到扰动后可诱导出与人类临床快感缺失一致的行为缺陷，为将神经科学范式迁移至AI可解释性研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language Models capture increasingly complex aspects of human cognition. Here we ask whether this alignment extends to reward valuation, which we assess in a mechanistic framework built on clinical tests that were developed to evaluate anhedonia and motivational deficits in major depressive disorder. In the brain, anhedonia is frequently linked to dysregulation in the Nucleus Accumbens (NAc) and the broader dopaminergic reward system. While neuroimaging has localized these deficits, establishing a causal link between NAc activity and specific behavioral symptoms remains a challenge. We use these ideas from neuroscience to functionally identify reward-anticipatory units in vision language models, and test their causal role via targeted perturbations. Perturbing NAc-selective units induces behavioral effects that mirror human anhedonia: the model shifts toward low-effort, low-reward options in effort-based decision-making tasks. Crucially, our results reflect a specific deficit in reward valuation and anticipation rather than a loss of task capability: the perturbed model maintains baseline performance when reward-based choice is removed. This induced vulnerability further aligns with clinical anhedonia and motivation scales, including DARS and MAP-SR. Taken together, these results reveal reward valuation circuits in AI models that parallel those in humans.

</details>


### [65] [Avoiding unsafe sets when training with Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07538)
*Adam M. Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强凸损失函数下的噪声梯度下降轨迹，论文研究了轨迹落入失败区域的概率。提出了三种界限：平衡态指数小概率、轨迹概率松弛到平衡态两倍的全局界限，以及基于局部谱测度的改进界限。通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck示例证明全局界限的预热时间是必要的，而局部速率可消除瞬态膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在噪声梯度下降训练中，轨迹可能进入失败区域，需要量化该概率以确保安全性。现有分析主要关注平衡态行为，而动态轨迹中的瞬态风险尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 将噪声梯度下降理想化为过阻尼Langevin动力学，在d维光滑强凸损失和能量间隙分离的失败区域设定下，利用全局谱隙、局部谱测度（而非Dirichlet型Rayleigh商）以及最大值原理推导概率上界。

Result: 1）平衡态概率π(A_H)随d指数衰减；2）轨迹概率ν_t(A_H) ≤ π(A_H)(1+√(χ₀²/π(A_H)) e^{-mt})，经d阶预热后松弛到静态值两倍；3）对几何隔离的失败区域，局部松弛率超过全局率，可成比例缩短预热时间，并均匀界住轨迹概率。

Conclusion: 强凸性决定了训练松弛的整体速率，但失败区域的几何形状决定了轨迹是否会中途"鼓胀"穿过该区域。局部谱测度方法比全局方法更精确地刻画瞬态风险。

Abstract: Training a model with noisy gradient descent can be idealized as overdamped Langevin dynamics on the loss landscape, and a natural safety question is to bound the probability $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) = \mathbb{P}(Q_t \in \mathcal{A}_H)$ that the trajectory lies in a designated failure region $\mathcal{A}_H$. We study this for a smooth, strongly convex loss in $d$ dimensions and a failure region separated from the minimizer by an energy gap. Three bounds emerge. At the end of training, the equilibrium mass $π(\mathcal{A}_H)$ is exponentially small in $d$, with a complementary energy-barrier rate when the noise is small. Along the trajectory, a shape-free bound $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le π(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{χ_0^2/π(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$ shows that the in-set probability relaxes to (twice) the static value after a burn-in time of order $d$, using only the global spectral gap $m$ of the loss. A worked Ornstein-Uhlenbeck example shows this burn-in is necessary: an angular slice of the equilibrium shell can transiently swell by a factor exponential in $d$, even though its equilibrium mass is tiny. To rule such swelling out we introduce a local relaxation rate attached to the failure region, defined through the spectral measure of its centered indicator rather than a Dirichlet-form Rayleigh quotient. For geometrically isolated regions this rate exceeds the global one, shrinking the burn-in proportionally, and combined with a maximum-principle ceiling it caps the trajectory probability uniformly in time. The picture is that strong convexity sets how fast training relaxes, but the shape of the unsafe set decides whether the trajectory bulges through it on the way home.

</details>


### [66] [Cross-Trajectory Chimera Interventions Reveal Dissociable Roles of Weight Magnitude and Direction in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06628)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将部分训练网络的方向成分（单位向量）移植到另一个独立训练网络中，能以阈值方式传递供体特定的电路身份，而范数成分仅传递延迟效应，无身份信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究部分训练网络的因果属性（如权重范数和方向）是否能在不同种子、独立训练的网络间跨轨迹迁移，而不仅仅是单轨干预中的必要性分析。

Method: 提出跨轨迹嵌合体干预方法：将两个不同种子训练的重量向量拆分为范数和单位方向，重组后继续训练；在两种模算术任务上验证，并采用自适应二分法定位阈值。

Result: 方向携带可迁移的供体特定电路身份，40/40例成功；范数仅产生分布式的延迟效应，无身份信号；转移具有阈值特性，阈值位置由受体范数完美预测。

Conclusion: 方向索引轨迹所趋近的解决方案，范数控制该身份被覆盖的易感性；部分训练网络中存在因果可跨轨迹移植的分离成分。

Abstract: Which properties of a partially trained network are causally portable to a different, independently trained network? Single-trajectory interventions show necessity within one run, not portability across runs. We introduce cross-trajectory chimera interventions: given two runs from different seeds, we split each weight vector into a norm and a unit direction, recombine one run's norm with the other's direction, and continue training. On two modular-arithmetic tasks that grok, the components dissociate. Direction carries a transferable, donor-specific circuit identity: implanting a donor's direction at the recipient's norm drives the run to the donor's circuit in 40/40 cases, while an angle-matched random control yields no shift. The transfer is threshold-like, and its location is predicted by the recipient's norm, separating perfectly by norm class over all 20 pairs (joint permutation probability 1.9e-4). Norm carries only a modest, distributed delay effect and no identity signal. An adaptive bisection procedure localizes the threshold to +/-1/64. Direction indexes which solution a trajectory approaches; norm governs how susceptible that identity is to being overwritten.

</details>


### [67] [STST-JEPA: Shallow-Target Spatio-Temporal Joint Embedding Prediction Architecture For EEG Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06629)
*Roy Segal,Yoni Svechinsky,Tomer Fekete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STST-JEPA，一种基于自监督学习的EEG基础模型，预训练于47,703个会话（年龄5-81岁），通过掩码预测与信号重构联合目标在30秒多通道窗口上学习。在年龄回归任务上达到3.06年MAE（r=0.924），并在NeuralBench榜单的性别分类、年龄预测和精神病理学回归任务中获得第一。模型年龄预测残差与认知效率负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG脑年龄模型面临跨站点导联配置异质性、标注样本少、个体水平非平稳性等挑战，且现有EEG基础模型难以在从儿童到老年人的全年龄段实现具有竞争力的年龄回归。本文旨在构建一个能够应对上述挑战、在全年龄段有效且可迁移的EEG基础模型。

Method: 采用自监督变换器（JEPA架构），结合潜在预测目标（对掩码token表示进行预测，目标来自tokenizer的EMA）与辅助信号重构项，在时空块掩码下的30秒多通道窗口上训练。预训练数据来自brain.space和HBN语料库共47,703个会话。下游使用冻结嵌入上的轻量级注意力探针进行年龄回归，或对最后几层进行任务特定微调。

Result: 年龄回归最佳MAE为3.06年（r=0.924），对比均值基线约10年MAE。在NeuralBench榜单上：性别分类平衡准确率0.911，年龄预测r=0.749，精神病理学复合回归r=0.215，均获第一。年龄预测残差与认知效率呈负相关。

Conclusion: STST-JEPA在全年龄段EEG年龄回归上表现优异，且预训练表征可有效迁移至性别分类、精神病理学预测等下游任务，年龄残差与认知效率的相关性进一步验证了其作为生物标志物的实用性。

Abstract: Brain age -- the age inferred from a physiological recording -- is an emerging biomarker whose deviation from chronological age tracks neurological and psychiatric burden, and EEG is an attractive substrate for it because it is cheap, portable, and temporally rich. Yet EEG brain-age models must contend with cross-site montage heterogeneity, small labelled cohorts, and dominant subject-level non-stationarity, and few EEG foundation models have been shown to deliver competitive age regression across the full pediatric-to-older-adult range in which such a biomarker would actually be deployed. We introduce STST-JEPA, a self-supervised transformer for resting-state and task EEG, pretrained on 47,703 sessions spanning ages 5-81 from the brain.space and Healthy Brain Network (HBN) corpora. The model combines a latent-prediction objective - predicting masked-token representations against an EMA-of-tokenizer target - with an auxiliary signal-reconstruction term, applied to 30-second multi-channel windows under spatiotemporal block masks. A lightweight attentive probe trained on frozen pretrained embeddings achieves a best held-out-validation mean absolute error of 3.06 years (r = 0.924) for age regression on 3,367 sessions, against a predict-the-mean baseline of approximately 10 years MAE. With light task-specific fine-tuning of the model's final layers, the same pretrained encoder achieves rank-1 placements - with the model's native 30-second windows - on the public NeuralBench x brain.space EEG leaderboard for sex classification (balanced accuracy 0.911), age prediction (r = 0.749), and psychopathology composite regression (r = 0.215). We further show that the model's age-prediction residual is negatively correlated with cognitive efficiency over several tasks we examined.

</details>


### [68] [When Certificates Fail: A Unified Safety Framework for Embedded Neural Interface Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06630)
*Jasmeet Singh Bindra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经接口模型中形式化鲁棒性证书与任务准确性之间存在根本性脱节：在EEGNet分类准确率下降25.7%的攻击下，Lipschitz证书仍对所有9名受试者有效。作者提出了包含三种对齐失败的统一实证审计框架——验证不足、代理-保真度分歧和潜在信息泄露——并证明运营安全审计而非仅证书验证对神经接口部署至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经接口系统中数学认证与操作安全性之间存在差距，训练目标偏离用户福祉，需要统一的实证审计框架来识别和量化这些对齐失败。

Method: 提出包含三种对齐失败（验证不足、代理-保真度分歧、潜在信息泄露）的统一实证审计框架，在BCI Competition IV 2a和SEED-IV数据集上使用多种深度/经典EEG解码器（EEGNet、CSP+LDA、FBCSP+LDA），通过官方session级验证、空控制和配对统计检验进行验证。

Result: 1) 验证不足：ε=0.25时EEGNet分类准确率下降25.7%，但Lipschitz证书对所有9名受试者仍有效，且该差距跨架构（EEGNet、CSP+LDA、FBCSP+LDA）存在。2) 代理-保真度分歧：时域辅助目标使重建MSE降低0.1132，但频谱log-MSE恶化。3) 潜在信息泄露：公共任务嵌入中受试者身份可恢复率达48.1%（vs 6.7%随机概率）。

Conclusion: 运营安全审计（而非仅证书验证）对负责任的神经接口部署是必要的。

Abstract: Formal robustness certificates for embedded neural-interface models can pass while task accuracy collapses: at perturbation budget e=0.25, EEGNet classification accuracy drops by 25.7% under projected-gradient attack while the Lipschitz-style certificate remains valid for all 9 tested subjects. We argue that this gap between mathematical certification and operational safety is one instance of a broader alignment failure in neural interfaces, where training objectives diverge from user welfare. We propose a unified empirical audit framework organized around three such failures: verification insufficiency, in which certificates pass while task behavior degrades; proxy-fidelity divergence, in which task-optimized representations damage neural signal structure (a time-domain auxiliary objective reduces reconstruction MSE by 0.1132 while worsening spectral log-MSE); and latent information exfiltration, in which public-task embeddings retain private attributes (subject identity recoverable at 48.1% versus 6.7% chance). We instantiate the framework on BCI Competition IV 2a and SEED-IV using multiple deep and classical EEG decoders, official session-level validation, null controls, and paired statistical tests. The verification gap persists across EEGNet, CSP+LDA, and FBCSP+LDA, and is therefore architecture-independent. Our results establish that operational safety auditing, not certificate verification alone, is necessary for responsible neural-interface deployment.

</details>
