The characteristics of heavy-tailed distributions are widely observed in real-world applications such as personalized recommendation, financial markets, and medical treatments. Based on the online mirror descent (OMD) method, we propose an algorithm EHM that extends the adaptive Huber loss method (Wang et al., 2025) with one-pass update ($\mathcal{O}(1)$ computational complexity with respect to current round $t$ and the time horizon $T$), which simultaneously achieves an almost optimal regret of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$ where $T$ is the time horizon. In addition, by utilizing a special property of some link function (Sawarni et al., 2025), our algorithm eliminates the need to know a commonly used parameter. Next, we study the GLB problem under the case when contextual characteristic becomes piecewise constant, and we slightly revised former algorithm to obtain the PGLB-EHM algorithm. After theoretical analysis, we prove that the regret upper bound order stays the same. Furthermore, we look deeper into a special case of nonlinear bandit (NB) and present the NB-EHM algorithm with bisection method and special restriction. Eventually we utilize the affine lifting approach and show that the general NB problem can be applied with NB-EHM to achieve a sublinear regret bound.

</details>


### [142] [Mechanistic Interpretability for Neural Networks: Circuits, Sparse Features and Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07316)
*Pranav Sawant,Jakub Krejčí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了机械可解释性领域，涵盖Transformer电路分析、叠加与多语义性问题、稀疏自编码器等工具，以及通过因果干预控制模型行为的方法，并探讨了与神经符号AI的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释AI方法仅停留于输入-输出层面的相关性分析，无法揭示神经网络内部算法机制，这在高风险部署场景下对安全性和可审计性构成重大挑战。机械可解释性旨在逆向工程神经网络的内部算法，直接解决模型"黑箱"问题。

Method: 详细剖析Transformer电路分析（残差流、注意力机制、归纳头等内部组件如何驱动复杂任务和上下文学习）；通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和transcoders应对叠加与多语义性挑战，将纠缠的网络激活分解为可解释特征；利用控制向量和因果干预主动控制和修改模型行为；将机械可解释性与神经符号AI框架结合，将神经表示翻译为显式可执行的逻辑规则。

Result: 系统梳理了机械可解释性领域的关键技术路线：从逆向工程Transformer内部电路，到利用SAEs分解叠加特征，再到因果干预控制模型行为，最终连接至神经符号AI实现可执行逻辑规则的翻译。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性通过逆向工程神经网络的内部算法，超越了传统可解释AI的局限性，为高风险场景下的模型安全性和可审计性提供了技术路径。叠加与多语义性问题的解决以及向显式逻辑规则的转化是该领域的重要发展方向。

Abstract: This article offers a comprehensive overview of mechanistic interpretability, an emerging field that seeks to reverse-engineer the internal algorithms of modern neural networks. While traditional explainable AI methods often stop at surface-level input-output correlations, this approach directly addresses the opaque "black box" nature of machine learning models, which is essential for ensuring safety and auditability in high-stakes deployments. The paper provides a detailed examination of Transformer circuit analysis, exploring how internal components like the residual stream, attention mechanisms, and induction heads drive complex tasks and in-context learning. It subsequently tackles the core challenge of superposition and polysemanticity, demonstrating how tools like Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) and transcoders can decompose tangled network activations into distinct, human-interpretable features. Furthermore, the paper explores methods for actively controlling and modifying model behavior through steering vectors and causal interventions. Finally, it connects these mechanistic insights with neurosymbolic AI frameworks designed to translate neural representations into explicit, executable logical rules.

</details>


### [143] [Generalist Vision-Language Models for Fast Radio Burst detection: a zero-shot benchmark against a specialized detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07382)
*Raiff H. Santos,Amilcar R. Queiroz,Tharcisyo S. S. Duarte,K. E. L. de Farias,Rafael A. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了小型、开源的通用视觉语言模型（VLMs）在零样本、仅提示词条件下检测快速射电暴（FRB）的能力，无需微调或标注数据。在2000个模拟动态频谱的基准测试中，Gemma 4 2B达到93.65%的准确率，与专业检测器SwinYNet（92.90%）无统计显著差异，且在结构化RFI上的假阳性率显著更低（6.4% vs 25.0%）。仅通过改写提示词，同一模型即可在三分类任务中达到86%准确率且无虚假FRB检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FRB检测依赖高度专业化的深度学习模型，这些模型需要大量任务特定的训练数据集且无法在不重新训练的情况下重新定义，因此探索是否可以利用小型通用视觉语言模型在零样本条件下完成FRB检测。

Method: 使用3000个模拟的L波段动态频谱（包含FRB、结构化RFI和噪声），从中抽取2000个样本构建平衡二分类基准。对比两个小型VLM（Gemma 4 2B和4B）与最先进专业检测器SwinYNet。VLMs在零样本、仅提示词条件下运行，无需微调和标注样本，返回结构化决策和自然语言解释。

Result: 在默认阈值下，Gemma 4 2B准确率93.65%，与SwinYNet的92.90%无统计显著差异；在结构化RFI上的假阳性率显著更低（6.4% vs 25.0%），且对纯噪声无假阳性。SwinYNet在概率排序上保持完美（ROC-AUC 1.0000 vs 0.9482）。仅改写提示词即可将同一模型用于三分类任务，在3000个完整数据集上达到86%准确率且无虚假FRB检测。

Conclusion: 小型通用视觉语言模型在零样本条件下能够达到与专业检测器相当的FRB检测性能，特别是在降低结构化RFI假阳性方面表现优异，且无需重新训练即可通过修改提示词重新配置任务，展示了通用模型在天文瞬变检测领域的潜力。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration radio transients whose automated detection increasingly relies on highly specialized deep learning models. These detectors achieve exceptional performance, but they require large task-specific training datasets and cannot be redefined without retraining. In this work, we evaluate whether small, open-weight, locally run generalist Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can detect FRBs in dynamic spectra under a zero-shot, prompt-only regime, with no fine-tuning and no labeled examples, returning structured decisions with a natural-language justification. From a controlled set of 3000 simulated L-band dynamic spectra containing FRBs, structured Radio Frequency Interference (RFI), and noise, we draw a balanced binary benchmark of 2000 samples and compare two such VLMs (Gemma 4 2B and 4B), sample by sample, against the state-of-the-art specialized detector SwinYNet. At the default threshold, Gemma 4 2B reaches an accuracy of 93.65%, with no statistically significant difference from SwinYNet (92.90%), while showing a significantly lower false-positive rate on structured RFI (6.4% vs. 25.0%) and no false positives on pure noise. SwinYNet retains a perfect probabilistic ranking on this benchmark (ROC-AUC of 1.0000 vs. 0.9482), a ceiling that the zero-shot VLM approaches from general-purpose pretraining alone. Rewriting the prompt alone reconfigures the same models for three-class FRB/RFI/noise classification on the full set of 3000 spectra, where they reach up to 86% accuracy without a single false FRB.

</details>


### [144] [Sparse Delta Memory: Scaling the State of Linear RNNs through Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07386)
*Loïc Cabannes,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Gergely Szilvasy,Matthijs Douze,Maria Lomeli,Ilze Amanda Auzina,Justin Carpentier,Gabriel Synnaeve,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse Delta Memory (SDM)架构，通过稀疏寻址方案将门控线性RNN的隐藏状态容量提升数个数量级，在等FLOP约束下显著改善长上下文召回和上下文学习性能，并可通过学习初始状态作为参数化记忆进一步提升推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型因固定状态大小导致长上下文召回性能不如softmax注意力Transformer；增大状态大小虽可提升召回但会带来更高FLOPs成本。作者旨在在不显著增加计算量的前提下提升线性注意力模型的记忆容量。

Method: 基于Gated DeltaNet架构，用稀疏读写替换密集的键值外积操作，引入大规模显式记忆作为状态存储，通过稀疏寻址方案实现高效访问。

Result: 在等FLOP约束和相同参数量下，更高状态记忆容量显著提升上下文学习和长上下文检索任务表现；通过学习SDM记忆的初始状态作为参数化记忆，在常识知识和推理任务上进一步改善。

Conclusion: SDM通过稀疏寻址扩展线性RNN状态容量而不显著增加计算成本，有效弥合了线性注意力与softmax注意力在长上下文任务上的差距。

Abstract: Linear attention models allow a fixed state size and a fixed amount of compute per token. However, due to their limited state size, linear attention models fall behind in long-context recall compared to softmax-attention-based transformer architectures. Increasing the state size of linear attention improves recall performance but at the cost of higher FLOPs. In this work, we introduce Sparse Delta Memory (SDM), an architecture that scales the hidden state of gated linear RNNs to orders of magnitude higher capacity using a sparse addressing scheme. SDM extends the Gated DeltaNet architecture by replacing the dense key-value outer product with sparse reads and writes to a large explicit memory. We show that, under an isoFLOP constraint and with an identical number of parameters, a higher state memory capacity significantly improves performance on in-context learning and long-context retrieval tasks. Moreover, by learning the initial state of the SDM memory and therefore using it as a parametric memory, we show that the model further improves on a wide range of common-knowledge and reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [145] [FourierQK: Spectral Preprocessing of Query-Key Projections Improves Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07478)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对注意力机制中的Query和Key投影进行FFT频谱预处理，在字符级语言建模任务上显著优于标准点积注意力。四个学习到的频率收敛到近几何级数的多尺度排序（49, 27, 10, 6 token/周期），对应段落、子段落、短语和单词尺度，将困惑度降低79%。该方法与FNet架构不同，仅预处理Q/K投影而保留完整注意力结构。因果滤波器无效，表明双边的非因果性是该方法的架构边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力在字符级语言建模中缺乏对多尺度频率结构的显式建模，而频谱域可提供更优的特征混合方式以捕捉从词到段落的不同层级依赖。

Method: 在标准Transformer注意力中，对Query和Key投影施加FFT频谱预处理（固定随机滤波或可学习的频率滤波），保持完整的注意力分数计算结构，仅修改Q/K的表示空间。

Result: 在TinyShakespeare上，4个可学习频率达到val=0.309，相比标准注意力（val=1.475）降低79%；频率收敛到(49, 27, 10, 6) token/周期的多尺度排序；随机投影无改善；因果滤波器无效。

Conclusion: 频谱预处理Q/K投影可在字符级建模中带来显著收益，受益来自全局频域混合而非度量扭曲；该方法具有内在非因果性，定义了与因果谱注意力的架构分界。

Abstract: FFT-based spectral preprocessing of learned query-key (Q/K) projections substantially improves transformer attention on character-level language modelling. On TinyShakespeare: a fixed random spectral filter achieves val=1.031 (Delta=+0.443); a single learned frequency at paragraph scale achieves val=0.608 (Delta=+0.867); and four learned frequencies spanning paragraph to word scale achieve val=0.309 (Delta=+1.166), a 79% reduction over standard dot-product attention. The single-frequency result is confirmed across three random seeds (mean val=0.236, std=0.019). The four frequencies converge to a near-geometric multi-scale ordering (49, 27, 10, 6 tokens/cycle) corresponding to paragraph, sub-paragraph, phrase, and word scales. The gain is specific to spectral preprocessing: random orthogonal and non-orthogonal projections of Q/K produce no measurable improvement, suggesting the benefit comes from global frequency-domain mixing rather than metric distortion. All results are verified by a shuffled-validation diagnostic against positional leakage. Causal filters (Gaussian, Mexican Hat, Morlet) do not improve over standard attention at character-level tokenisation: the bilateral FFT kernel is structurally non-causal, coupling every position to future tokens. This defines an architectural boundary between bilateral spectral attention (this paper) and genuinely causal spectral attention at word-scale tokenisation (companion paper MorletQK). This work is architecturally distinct from FNet (Lee-Thorp et al., 2021), which replaces attention with Fourier mixing of token embeddings. Here, spectral preprocessing applies only to Q/K projections while the full attention score structure is preserved.

</details>


### [146] [Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07519)
*Ricardo Baptista,Olivier Zahm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRiLS（无梯度黎曼朗之万采样器），一种无需目标密度梯度计算即可在多模态概率分布中高效采样的MCMC方法，通过黎曼度量重塑局部几何结构促进模态间跳转，实验表明混合性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCMC方法在多模态概率分布中易陷入局部模式，混合效率差，且现有梯度方法在目标密度梯度不可用或计算昂贵时受限。

Method: 提出GRiLS：引入黎曼度量重塑局部几何以促进跨模态转移，无需梯度评估；利用交互粒子集合估计目标密度均值和协方差，构造无梯度黎曼朗之万采样器。

Result: 在多模态基准测试上，GRiLS的混合效率优于现有的基于梯度和无梯度MCMC方法。

Conclusion: GRiLS为复杂、计算昂贵的多模态目标分布提供了一种有效的无梯度采样替代方案。

Abstract: We address the problem of efficiently sampling multimodal probability distributions, where standard Markov Chain Monte Carlo methods often suffer from poor mixing and mode trapping. To mitigate these issues, we propose Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler (GRiLS), a novel proposal that improves exploration without requiring gradient evaluations of the target density. Our approach introduces a Riemannian metric which reshapes the local geometry in order to facilitate transitions across modes. The resulting gradient-free MCMC algorithm is particularly suitable for complex, computationally expensive targets where derivatives are unavailable or impractical. The GRiLS proposal requires knowing the mean and covariance of the target density, which we estimate using an ensemble of interacting particles. Empirical results on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that GRiLS achieves improved mixing compared to existing gradient-based and gradient-free MCMC approaches.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-Class vs. Multi-Label BERT for CVE-to-CWE Mapping: How Taxonomy Structure Shapes the Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07573)
*Ana Schwengber Kelm,Christian Bockermann,Jörg Frochte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将CVE记录自动分类为CWE类别的文本分类任务，比较了多类（单标签）和多标签两种建模方法，在三种Transformer编码器和三个嵌套标签空间上的表现。结果表明多类训练在所有设置下宏F1更高，但随标签空间缩小差距减小；混淆模式主要由CWE层次结构驱动，而非编码器选择；层次松弛评估显著提升F1；CySecBERT总体表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVE记录分配CWE类别的过程目前主要依赖人工操作，效率低下且成本高昂。本研究旨在通过文本分类方法实现自动化CWE分配，以提升漏洞分析的效率和准确性。

Method: 将CWE分类任务建模为文本分类问题，对比多类（单标签）和多标签两种形式；使用BERT Base、SecureBERT和CySecBERT三种Transformer编码器；在83、47、25三种嵌套标签空间上评估；采用后验阈值优化和层次松弛评估等方法分析模型表现。

Result: 多类训练在所有设置下宏F1更高，但与多标签的差距从21个百分点缩小至2个百分点；25类设置下后验阈值优化可消除差距；三种编码器的混淆模式高度一致（Pearson r>0.92）；层次松弛评估将宏F1从约81%提升至约90%；CySecBERT在多标签设置下表现最佳且统计显著。

Conclusion: CWE自动分类任务中，多类建模整体优于多标签，但差距随标签空间缩小而减小；错误结构主要由CWE层次设计驱动而非编码器选择；层次松弛评估表明严格指标低估了分支级性能；CySecBERT是最优编码器选择。

Abstract: Assigning Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) records remains an important but largely manual step in vulnerability analysis. We study this task as a text classification problem and compare two modelling choices: a \emph{multi-class} formulation that predicts a single CWE per CVE and a \emph{multi-label} formulation that allows multiple assignments. Three transformer encoders (BERT Base, SecureBERT, and CySecBERT) are evaluated on three nested label spaces (83, 47, and 25 classes). Multi-class training achieves higher macro-F1 across all settings, although the gap to multi-label narrows from 21 to 2 percentage points as the label space shrinks. Post-hoc threshold optimisation on the multi-label side closes this gap on the 25-class setting. Confusion analysis shows that the dominant misclassification patterns follow the CWE hierarchy and are shared across all three encoders (Pearson $r > 0.92$), which suggests that the error structure is driven more by taxonomy design than by encoder choice. A hierarchy-relaxed evaluation that forgives within-family confusions raises macro-F1 from ${\sim}$81\% to ${\sim}$90\%, indicating that strict metrics understate branch-level classifier quality. CySecBERT achieves the strongest results overall, with statistically significant gains concentrated in the multi-label setting.

</details>


### [148] [Asymmetric Focal Loss Improves Graph Neural Network Prediction of Drug-Drug Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07611)
*Faranak Hatami,Mousa Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClinicalFocal loss作为非对称焦点损失函数，替代标准二值交叉熵损失训练图神经网络预测药物-药物相互作用。在TWOSIDES数据集上，准确率从0.699提升至0.892（+19.3%），F1从0.700提升至0.894（+19.4%），假阴性率从29.8%降至9.1%，分类误差相对降低64.1%，表明损失函数设计是提升图基DDI预测性能的直接可调杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二值交叉熵训练对已正确分类样本和困难样本分配相同容量，可能遗漏临床显著的药物相互作用，需要一种能够强调困难正例的非对称优化目标来提升多关系DDI预测性能。

Method: 将ClinicalFocal loss（非对称焦点损失）集成到关系感知图卷积网络中，使用分子指纹、理化描述符和学习嵌入作为特征，在TWOSIDES数据集上采用五折交叉验证，与二值交叉熵基线保持完全相同的实验条件（架构、特征、数据划分、超参数和随机种子）。

Result: 准确率从0.699升至0.892（+19.3%），F1从0.700升至0.894（+19.4%），AUROC从0.766升至0.914，AUCPR从0.714升至0.860，假阴性率从29.8%降至9.1%，特异性从69.6%升至87.5%，分类误差从30.1%降至10.8%（相对降低64.1%），所有五折结果一致改善。

Conclusion: 非对称焦点优化在不修改底层架构的情况下提升了分类和排序性能，对观测到的交互三元组实现了90.9%的召回率。损失函数设计是改进图基DDI预测的直接、可调杠杆。

Abstract: Background: Graph neural networks improve computational prediction of polypharmacy side effects, but standard binary cross-entropy training allocates equal capacity to well-classified and difficult examples, potentially missing clinically significant interactions. We evaluated whether an asymmetric focal objective could improve multi-relational drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction by emphasizing difficult positive interactions. Methods: ClinicalFocal loss was integrated into a relation-aware graph convolutional network using molecular fingerprints, physicochemical descriptors, and learned embeddings. The model was evaluated on TWOSIDES using five-fold cross-validation with identical experimental conditions (architecture, features, data partitions, hyperparameters, and random seeds) for ClinicalFocal loss and binary cross-entropy baseline. Results: ClinicalFocal loss increased accuracy from 0.699 to 0.892 (+19.3 percentage points) and F1 score from 0.700 to 0.894 (+19.4 percentage points). AUROC increased from 0.766 to 0.914, and AUCPR increased from 0.714 to 0.860. The false-negative rate decreased from 29.8% to 9.1%, while specificity increased from 69.6% to 87.5%. Overall classification error decreased from 30.1% to 10.8%, corresponding to a 64.1% relative reduction. Improvements were consistent across all five folds. Conclusions: Asymmetric focal optimization improved classification and ranking performance while achieving 90.9% recall for observed interaction triples, without modifying the underlying architecture. Loss-function design is a direct, tunable lever for improving graph-based DDI prediction.

</details>


### [149] [Higher-Order Geometric Updates for Levenberg-Marquardt Method via Riemann Normal Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07623)
*Jianing Liu,Dong H. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出黎曼正规坐标Levenberg-Marquardt方法(RNC-LM)，通过构造有限步更新的RNC曲线消除参数效应曲率，在经典非线性最小二乘基准、反应扩散PINN失效模式基准和大规模机器学习势能面拟合任务上均显著优于标准LM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LM方法的切空间步在参数坐标中作为直线更新，无法消除参数效应曲率；测地线加速度仅在无穷小步长极限下精确消除该曲率，有限步长时存在不一致性。

Method: RNC-LM重新表述测地线方程，将测地线加速度推广到任意阶修正，构造具有渐进更高重参数化一致性的有限步更新；沿RNC曲线进行线搜索控制步长，在移动切向帧中逐阶消除残差加速度的切向分量。

Result: 在经典弯曲谷和秩亏问题中改善收敛性和鲁棒性；在反应扩散PINN基准上将相对L2误差降至1e-3量级并恢复物理有意义的解；在大规模机器学习势能面拟合任务中实现比标准LM快34倍的加速。

Conclusion: RNC-LM通过有限步的几何一致性改进了LM方法，在保持接近LM计算成本的同时显著提升优化性能，特别适用于具有强参数效应曲率的非线性最小二乘问题。

Abstract: Nonlinear least-squares optimization is central to regression, physics-informed neural networks, and other machine-learning tasks. Such problems have a natural geometric interpretation, model predictions form a manifold in data space, while the chosen parameterization can introduce parameter-effects curvature that becomes a dominant source of nonlinearity. This exposes a limitation of the Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) method, its tangent-space step is applied as a straight update in parameter coordinates. Geodesic acceleration gives a second-order correction, but its removal of parameter-effect curvature is exact only in the infinitesimal-step limit. We propose a Riemann-normal-coordinate Levenberg-Marquardt method (RNC-LM) to improve this consistency for finite optimization steps. By reformulating the geodesic equation, RNC-LM extends geodesic acceleration to arbitrary-order corrections and constructs finite-step updates with progressively higher reparameterization consistency. A line search along the resulting RNC curve controls the traveled distance while keeping the cost close to standard LM. The method eliminates the tangential component of residual acceleration order by order in a moving tangent frame, making the actual objective reduction more consistent with the linear model prediction of LM. On classical nonlinear least-squares benchmarks, RNC-LM improves convergence and robustness in curved valleys and rank-deficient problems. On a reaction-diffusion PINN failure-mode benchmark, it reduces the relative L2 error to the order of 1e-3 and recovers a physically meaningful solution. On a large-scale machine-learning potential-energy-surface fitting task, it achieves a 34-fold speedup over standard LM.

</details>


### [150] [Guidance Breaks the Fitted Operator: A Terminal-Fitted Repair for Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07665)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过渐近保持数值分析视角，证明无分类器引导（CFG）在强引导条件下会使DDIM步长的残差发散，导致过饱和与不稳定。提出修复方案：将引导项从 w(r-1) 替换为 r^(1+w)-r，该单系数修正无需额外NFE，在CIFAR-10和Stable Diffusion 1.5上可稳定高引导、消除饱和，并在9/9网格点上超越CFG的FID指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在大引导系数下会出现过饱和和不稳定现象，现有方法只能通过增加步数或限制时间窗口来缓解，缺乏理论分析和根本性解决方案。

Method: 采用渐近保持数值分析视角，基于DDIM步长是无引导终端层的唯一拟合算子这一结论，证明引导会使判别子空间"重新变硬"至异常指数1+w，导致DDIM不再适用且残差随 sigma_min→0 发散。提出将CFG的 w(r-1) 替换为 r^(1+w)-r 的单系数修正。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM上，该修正消除了sigma_min发散的爆破，在9/9测试网格点上FID全面优于CFG，同时保持分类器代理目标精度。

Conclusion: 该修正是一个高引导稳定器而非通用质量旋钮，能减少残差放大和饱和，但并非普遍性图像质量提升方案，也不是对密集CFG参考场的统一更优积分器。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the standard way to strengthen class-conditioning in diffusion and flow-matching samplers, yet at large guidance it oversaturates and destabilizes, symptoms practitioners suppress with more steps or limited-interval schedules. We analyze CFG through an asymptotic-preserving, numerical-analysis lens. Building on a recent result that the deterministic DDIM step is the unique fitted operator for the unguided terminal layer, exact on the final small-sigma stretch of sampling, we show that guidance re-stiffens exactly the discriminative subspace to an anomalous exponent 1+w. DDIM is therefore no longer fitted there, and on coarse meshes its guided residual diverges as sigma_min goes to zero. We prove a guided clock barrier with three ordered step-size thresholds, and read one-step oversaturation as its endpoint: a solver artifact on the calibration model rather than the continuous guided law. The same analysis yields a one-coefficient, zero-extra-NFE repair: replace CFG's w(r-1) by r^(1+w)-r on the guidance direction. On the calibration model's discriminative crossover, this removes CFG's sigma_min-divergent blow-up and is first-order accurate against the exact guided flow as sigma_min goes to zero. On learned CIFAR-10 checkpoints, and as a cross-domain smoke test on Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM, it acts as a high-guidance stabilizer at no extra cost rather than a universal quality knob: it cuts residual amplification and saturation, gives 9/9 point-FID wins over CFG on the tested grid, and preserves classifier-proxy target accuracy in the hard-cell blocks. We report the limits alongside: it is not a universal image-quality win, and against a dense vanilla-CFG reference it is not a uniformly better integrator of that field.

</details>


### [151] [PeTeR: Post-Training Robustification of Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07671)
*Adrian Ciotinga,Yeming Dai,YooJung Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PeTeR，一种无需数据和重新训练的后训练框架，用于增强预训练概率电路对分布偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于似然的概率电路学习在面对数据噪声、小样本或分布偏移时容易过拟合和泛化脆弱，现有的分布鲁棒优化方法需要从头训练模型，成本高且不灵活。

Method: 提出PeTeR（Post-training Robustification），一种无需数据的后训练框架，通过对预训练PC进行后处理来增强其对分布偏移的鲁棒性，避免了从零开始的重新训练。

Result: 在多个密度估计基准上，PeTeR有效提升了基线模型对随机和对抗扰动的鲁棒性，性能达到或超过了需要数据的鲁棒学习方法。

Conclusion: PeTeR是一种高效且有效的后训练鲁棒化方法，在不依赖数据且无需重训的条件下显著提升概率电路的分布鲁棒性。

Abstract: Probabilistic circuits (PCs) can model complex joint distributions while supporting exact and efficient computation of many inference queries. However, standard likelihood-based PC learning is vulnerable to overfitting and fragile generalization when confronted with data noise, small sample sizes, or distribution shifts. This can be mitigated using distributionally-robust optimization which consider worst-case distributions within a Wasserstein ball of the empirical distribution, but current methods are limited to training a model from scratch in this framework. Instead, we propose PeTeR: a novel, data-free post-training framework designed to robustify pre-trained PCs against distribution shifts without retraining from scratch. Empirical evaluations across multiple density estimation benchmarks demonstrate that PeTeR effectively robustifies baseline models against both random and adversarial perturbations, achieving competitive or superior performance to data-dependent robust learning baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Max Out GRPO Signal: Adaptive Trace Prefix Control for Hard Reasoning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07674)
*Vladislav Beliaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO在模型最难的题目上会因无成功样本导致梯度消失，AdaPrefix-GRPO通过将正确答案前缀作为难度调节旋钮，并利用反馈控制器将成功率维持在50%附近以最大化梯度信号，最终撤除辅助，在匹配计算量下使基础模型准确率提升1.6-2.1倍，同时追踪长度减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GRPO在模型最难问题上因组内均无成功轨迹而导致优势归零、梯度消失的问题，使模型能从最需要学习的前沿样本中获得有效学习信号。

Method: 提出AdaPrefix-GRPO：将参考解的正确前缀作为难度调节旋钮，通过反馈控制器在训练过程中动态调整每个题目所需前缀长度，使该题成功率维持在50%附近（梯度信号最大处），训练完成后完全撤除前缀辅助。

Result: 在匹配训练FLOPs条件下，0.6B模型在训练分布内保留问题上准确率提升2.1倍，Qwen3-1.7B提升1.6倍，AIME提升1.7倍，追踪长度约减半；模型越小增益越大。

Conclusion: AdaPrefix-GRPO是一种轻量级（仅需数据准备和前缀token损失掩码）、高效的方法，通过动态课程学习策略显著提升GRPO在困难数学问题上的训练效果和推理效率。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) stalls on a model's hardest problems: when no rollout in a group succeeds, the group-relative advantages vanish and the problem contributes no gradient, wasting the frontier examples we most want to learn from. Prepending a correct prefix of a reference solution raises the success rate, making prefix length a continuous knob on difficulty. Concurrent methods set the knob once; AdaPrefix-GRPO turns it into a feedback controller: throughout training it adjusts how much of the solution each problem gets, holding its success rate near 50%, where GRPO's gradient signal is largest, then withdraws the assistance entirely, so the deployed model solves problems unaided. On hard math, at matched training FLOPs, it more than doubles GRPO's accuracy on held-out problems from the training distribution for a 0.6B model (2.1x), with 1.6x on Qwen3-1.7B and 1.7x on AIME, while roughly halving trace length. The method is implemented in data preparation plus a loss mask on prefix tokens; the trainer is otherwise stock. The smaller the model, the larger the gain.

</details>


### [153] [How Data Shapes RoPE Frequency Usage: From Positional Scale Matching to Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07678)
*Xinyi Wu,Siyuan Liu,Ali Jadbabaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频率匹配原理：RoPE位置编码中，每个频率作为一个“位置透镜”，最优频率由训练数据的依赖宽度决定（频率∝1/宽度）。研究发现语言模型中的中低频带来源于自然语言的多尺度依赖结构，而位置插值实现的长上下文泛化依赖于自然语言的近似自相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释RoPE位置编码中模型对固定频率网格的非均匀使用现象，揭示其背后的数据驱动机制。

Method: 提出频率匹配原理（频率∝1/依赖宽度），通过场-分辨率权衡形式化每个频率作为位置透镜的作用，在合成数据和文本数据上验证该原理。

Result: 模型频率使用由训练数据的依赖结构决定；语言模型的中低频带反映自然语言的多尺度依赖；长上下文泛化依赖两种尺度匹配：训练时频率与依赖宽度的匹配，以及频率缩放与依赖结构向长上下文扩展方式的匹配。

Conclusion: RoPE频率选择是数据驱动的结果，自然语言的近似自相似性支撑了测试时频率缩放以实现长上下文泛化的有效性。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) provide transformers with a fixed grid of positional frequencies, yet trained models use these frequencies highly non-uniformly. We study what determines this frequency usage and propose a data-centered explanation: RoPE frequencies are selected to match the relative-distance structure of the training data. Viewing each frequency as a positional lens, we formalize a field-resolution tradeoff and show that, for a data-induced dependency profile of width $W$, the optimal frequency scales as $1/W$. This frequency-matching principle explains controlled observations on synthetic and text-based data, and suggests that the mid-low frequency bands observed in language models arise from the multi-scale dependency structure of natural language. We further connect frequency selection to position-interpolation-based length generalization: scaling frequencies down expands the effective field while reducing resolution. This helps when longer-context dependencies are approximate dilations of those seen during training, but can fail when relevant dependencies do not scale with context length. Empirically, we show that natural language exhibits approximate self-similarity across positional scales, explaining why test-time frequency scaling can support long-context generalization. Overall, our results identify a data-driven mechanism behind emergent RoPE frequency usage and show that long-context generalization depends on two forms of scale matching: between learned frequencies and training-time dependencies, and between frequency scaling and how those dependencies extend to longer contexts.

</details>


### [154] [Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy for PDE-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07682)
*Xiangming Huang,Guannan Zhang,Lu Lu,Raphaël Pestourie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOTES框架，将DeepONet神经算子与CMA-ES进化策略结合，通过降维(256→25)和拓扑感知潜空间实现高效、可迁移的逆向设计，在纳米光子束偏转器(效率>95%)和结构优化(柔度达246)任务中超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程支配的物理系统逆向设计面临设计空间高维非凸的挑战；现有生成模型鲁棒性和可迁移性不足，而进化策略虽鲁棒但难以处理高维空间。需一种兼具降维、表征学习和鲁棒全局优化的方法。

Method: NOTES将DeepONet神经算子与CMA-ES协方差矩阵自适应进化策略耦合：先通过DeepONet学习拓扑先验构建紧凑潜空间，再在该低维空间执行CMA-ES全局优化；DeepONet的拓扑学习与PDE求解器中的物理规律解耦。

Result: 纳米光子束偏转器设计：维度从256降至25，效率持续超过95%，优于CMA-ES、拓扑优化等基线；结构优化：发现柔度低至246的设计。

Conclusion: NOTES通过解耦拓扑学习与物理求解，提供了灵活可迁移的逆向设计框架，在保证鲁棒性的同时显著提升了高维设计空间的优化效率。

Abstract: The inverse design of physical systems governed by partial differential equations is computationally demanding due to the high dimensionality and non-convexity of design spaces. Generative models for inverse design often lack robustness and transferability, whereas evolutionary strategies are robust but struggle in high-dimensional spaces. This paper introduces a Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy (NOTES) that integrates dimensionality reduction, representation learning, and evolutionary optimization for efficient and transferable inverse design. NOTES couples a DeepONet-based neural operator with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES) to perform global optimization in a compact latent space that encodes topology-aware priors while discovering high-performance designs for unseen operating conditions. Applied to nanophotonic beam-deflector inverse design governed by Maxwell's equations, NOTES reduces the design dimensionality from 256 to 25 and consistently achieves over 95 percent efficiency, outperforming CMA-ES, topology optimization, and other baselines. Applied to structural optimization, NOTES discovers designs that achieve compliance down to 246. By decoupling topology learning of a DeepONet from the governing physics in a PDE solver, NOTES provides a flexible and transferable framework for the inverse design of physical systems.

</details>


### [155] [ECGLight: Compute-Light Framework For Paper ECG Digitization and Myocardial Infarction Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07683)
*Shreyasvi Natraj,Cyrus Achtari,Felice Gragnano,Andrea Milzi,Marco Valgimigli,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、端到端的纸版心电图数字化-诊断流水线，通过智能手机拍摄纸版ECG，在仅CPU条件下30秒内完成信号重建和心肌梗死筛查，准确率达95.51%，解决了偏远地区纸版ECG无法被AI辅助诊断的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏远诊所仍广泛使用纸版ECG，因缺乏计算资源和高速网络，这些纸质记录无法被现有AI辅助诊断系统利用，导致急性冠脉闭塞等病症被延误诊治。现有工作将数字化和诊断分离处理，缺乏一个计算轻量、可在设备端运行的高保真纸版ECG重建与多临床终点诊断框架。

Method: 构建端到端的轻量级设备端数字化-诊断流水线：将智能手机拍摄的纸版ECG照片/扫描件转换为校准的12导联信号，进行心肌梗死病理筛查，并引入SHAP可解释性分析。在PTB-XL数据集（21,799份ECG）上训练评估，并在医院获取的ECG-Matrix数据集上进一步验证。

Result: 系统在仅CPU资源上每份ECG处理时间<30秒；PTB-XL上MI检测准确率95.51%（F1=0.9519）；ECG-Matrix上OMI检测准确率88.89%（F1=0.8862）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，在缺乏数字ECG导出、网络连接或高端计算资源的条件下，历史纸版记录可被可靠地数字化并普及应用，为全球偏远地区提供可扩展的决策支持。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is one of the most widely used tests for diagnosing cardiovascular disease. Yet several remote clinics still utilize paper ECG printouts for their analysis due to limited connectivity and computational capacity. As a result, vast numbers of physical ECGs obtained in remote areas still remain incapable of being accessed by contemporary artificial-intelligence (AI)-based decision support as they require high computational resources or strong high-speed internet connectivity. This causes several cases where conditions like acute coronary occlusion (ACS) is overlooked and reperfusion therapy delayed. Although prior work has tackled digitization and diagnosis separately, and utilized advanced AI models for them, there still remains a lack of a compute-light, on-device framework that reconstructs paper ECGs at high fidelity, while accurately supporting multiple clinically relevant endpoints. We address this need with an end-to-end lightweight on-device digitization-to-diagnosis pipeline that converts a smartphone photo or scan of a paper ECG into a calibrated 12-lead signal and screens for Myocardial Infarction (MI) pathologies, with SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to support interpretability. Trained and evaluated on 21,799 ECGs from the PTB-XL dataset and further validated on hospital-acquired ECG-Matrix dataset, the complete system runs in <30 s per ECG on CPU-only resources, achieving 95.51% accuracy (F1 = 0.9519) for MI detection on PTB-XL and 88.89% accuracy (F1 = 0.8862) for OMI detection on ECG-Matrix. This work showcases that legacy paper records can be reliably democratized in any part of the world, providing a scalable decision support when digital ECG export, connectivity, or high-end compute are unavailable

</details>


### [156] [The Key to Going Linear: Analysis-Driven Transformer Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07706)
*Anna Kuzina,Paul N. Whatmough,Babak Ehteshami Bejnordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在冻结骨干网络条件下，将因果自注意力线性化的方法，揭示了softmax依赖关键驱动的秩-1正交投影，并通过引入sink tokens、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由等结构干预，在高达32B参数的LLaMA和Qwen模型上超越了先前的后验线性化基线，匹配了复杂自适应缓存框架的长上下文检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果自注意力的二次计算成本严重制约了长上下文Transformer推理的性能瓶颈，现有后验线性化方法众多但难以判断哪些组件能保持模型质量。

Method: 在严格冻结骨干网络条件下隔离状态更新设计的影响，证明softmax依赖关键驱动的秩-1正交投影，引入sink tokens、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由等结构干预减少近似误差。

Result: 在LLaMA和Qwen模型（最高32B参数）上，该方法在MMLU基准上超越先前后验基线，并匹配复杂自适应缓存框架的长上下文检索性能。

Conclusion: delta风格网络优于纯门控累积的原因在于其利用了关键驱动的正交投影，所提结构干预能有效缩小线性化方法与原始softmax注意力之间的差距。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of causal self-attention severely bottlenecks long-context transformer inference. While numerous post hoc linearization pipelines exist, it is difficult to identify which components preserve model quality. This work isolates the effect of state update design in a strict frozen-backbone regime. We show that softmax relies on key-dependent, rank-1 orthogonal projections, elucidating why delta-style networks outperform purely gated accumulation. We identify a potential source of approximation errors and introduce structural interventions, specifically sink tokens, short convolutions, and fixed-budget cache routing, which reduces the remaining gap. We scale this linearization approach across LLaMA and Qwen models up to 32B parameters, outperforming prior post hoc baselines on MMLU and matching the long-context retrieval of complex adaptive-caching frameworks.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

### [157] [Value of Information under Imprecise Probabilities: Decision-Rule-Specific Values and Fixed-Measure Envelopes on a Credal Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06570)
*Rowan Iskandar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在概率测度不确定（即信度集）条件下，信息价值（VOI）分析需要重新定义。提出了两种VOI形式：一是固定决策规则（如Gamma-maximin）的规则特定VOI，二是覆盖所有精确测度的固定测度包络。证明了完美信息期望值在信度集上是凹函数，其下端点可由生成元精确获得，而上端点需通过线性规划计算。Gamma-maximin值可能超出整个包络范围。给出了基于标准估计器和信度集搜索的计算流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VOI分析假设单一概率测度，但实际中证据通常只能将测度确定到一个集合（信度集）。该假设不成立时，VOI需要重新定义和推导。

Method: 形式化区分了规则特定VOI（固定决策规则如Gamma-maximin）和固定测度包络（遍历所有精确测度）。分别推导了完美、部分和样本信息下这两种VOI的性质，利用凹性、线性规划和连续性界进行分析。

Result: 完美信息期望值在信度集上是凹函数；下端点由生成元精确获得，上端点可能位于内部需线性规划计算；Gamma-maximin值可能超出包络端点；给出了部分和样本信息端点可从生成元获取的条件；提供了结合标准估计器和信度集搜索的计算方法。

Conclusion: 在测度不确定条件下，两种VOI形式能够区分对所有可采纳测度都成立的结论与依赖于特定测度选择的结论，为实际决策提供了更稳健的分析框架。

Abstract: Value-of-information (VOI) analysis is usually conducted under a single probability measure. However, in practice, the available evidence often pins the measure down only to a set. Consequently, under a set of probability measures, VOI requires different formulations. First, we explicate a rule-specific VOI that fixes a decision rule for acting under imprecision (such as Gamma-maximin) and measures what the information is worth to a decision maker who uses that rule. Second, we derive a fixed-measure envelope that evaluates the classical VOI functional over all admissible precise measures. We formalize this distinction and explicate its consequences for the expected perfect, partial, and sample information. The expected value of perfect information is concave over the credal set. Hence, when the set is generated by finitely many measures, its lower envelope endpoint is obtained exactly from the generators, while its upper endpoint may be interior and is computed by a finite linear program. The Gamma-maximin value, in contrast, can exceed the entire envelope, so a rule-specific value is not recovered from the envelope's endpoints. A continuity bound limits how much the VOI can change as the measure varies, and we identify when the partial- and sample-information endpoints can still be obtained from the generators. Because the single-measure VOI must itself be estimated, the procedure we give combines standard estimators for it with a search over the credal set. By using a worked decision problem, we show how the two quantities separate conclusions that hold across every admissible measure from conclusions that depend on one unidentified choice of measure.

</details>


### [158] [Fast determinantal sampling on general spaces and diffusion geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06644)
*Hoang-Son Tran,Pranav Gupta,Subhroshekhar Ghosh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为行列式点过程（DPP）采样在非欧几里得空间（黎曼流形、加权网络）中建立了显式速率保证，达到了与欧氏空间相匹配的$(\text{样本大小})^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2d_{\text{int}}}}$收敛速率，其中$d_{\text{int}}$为数据的本征维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPP采样被认为比独立同分布采样具有更好的近似性质，且能部署于非常一般的空间。然而，以往的DPP理论分析主要集中在欧几里得空间，本文旨在将DPP采样的显式速率保证扩展到更一般的空间（如黎曼流形和加权网络），并建立与欧氏空间可比的收敛率。

Method: 利用由拉普拉斯算子及其他马尔可夫扩散算子的特征空间导出的谱核，建立DPP采样在紧黎曼流形和加权网络（k近邻图、随机几何图）上的理论保证。技术工具包括Weyl渐近律、马尔可夫扩散理论、Dirichlet形式以及伪微分算子理论。

Result: 在紧黎曼流形上，DPP采样率自动捕捉数据的本征维度$d_{\text{int}}$，达到$(\text{样本大小})^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2d_{\text{int}}}}$的收敛速率。在k近邻图和加权随机几何图上，同样实现了依赖于本征维度的改进速率，与欧氏空间已知速率相匹配。

Conclusion: 本文首次将DPP采样的显式速率保证从欧氏空间推广到黎曼流形和加权网络等一般空间，证明了DPP采样在更广泛数据表示中的理论优势，相关技术工具（Weyl律、Dirichlet形式等）在该领域具有独立价值。

Abstract: Determinantal point processes have recently emerged as a kernel-based alternative to standard independent sampling for constructing efficient minibatches, coresets, and other compact representations of large-scale datasets. In particular, sampling mechanisms based on DPPs are believed to demonstrate better approximation properties compared to classical i.i.d. samplers, even at the scale of the exponent. One of the key strengths of DPP based samplers is that they can be deployed over very general spaces, in contrast to more classical sampling methods beyond i.i.d. which tend to work in very well-structured settings, principally Euclidean spaces. In this work, we establish explicit rate guarantees for determinantal sampling in spaces that extend far beyond known Euclidean setups, focusing on spectral kernels obtained from eigenspaces of naturally associated Laplacian and other Markov diffusion operators. This includes, in particular, Riemannian manifolds and weighted networks. In determinantal sampling from compact Riemannian manifolds, we establish sampling rates that automatically pick up the intrinsic dimensionality $d_{\text{int}}$ of the underlying manifold. In the setting of networks, we investigate DPP-based samplers on the celebrated k-nearest neighbour graphs, as well as weighted random geometric graphs, and demonstrate a similar improved dependence on the intrinsic dimensionality of the data. Overall, our approach achieves guarantees of $\big(\text{sample size}\big)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2d_{\text{int}}}}$ that match known rates on Euclidean spaces of comparable dimension. In terms of techniques, we connect to the celebrated Weyl's Law for manifold spectra, and leverage tools from the theory of Markov diffusions and Dirichlet forms as well as certain ingredients from the theory of pseudodifferential operators, which could be of independent interest in this area.

</details>


### [159] [Heat-Kernel Entropy Profiles and Geometric Effective Sample Size for Weighted Measures on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06696)
*Kisung You*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出热核熵轮廓（heat-kernel entropy profiles），一种在紧致流形上基于内在热流扩散加权原子、跨尺度追踪非均匀性的多尺度摘要方法。针对二阶Rényi熵，该轮廓可从成对热核重叠计算，得到几何有效样本量，能对邻近或重复粒子进行折扣处理。论文证明了单调性、小尺度和大尺度渐近性、确定性权重一致性以及紧致无边流形上的有界比率自归一化重要性采样扩展。在球面上，未取对数轮廓可分解为球谐能量，恢复均值方向、von Mises-Fisher型和Bingham型摘要。球面实验表明，该轮廓能揭示仅用权重和一阶矩球面摘要无法捕捉的对跖、环带、多模态和重复粒子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仅依赖权重的有效样本量摘要（如普通有效样本量）忽略了支撑集的几何结构。在紧致流形上的重要性采样、粒子近似、后验总结、求积和表示学习等应用中，需要一种能同时考虑权重和空间几何信息的多尺度摘要方法。

Method: 利用热核在紧致流形上的内在热流扩散加权原子，构建跨尺度的热核熵轮廓。针对二阶Rényi熵，通过成对热核重叠计算轮廓，定义几何有效样本量。

Result: 证明了热核熵轮廓的单调性、小尺度和大尺度渐近性、确定性权重一致性以及有界比率自归一化重要性采样扩展。在球面上，轮廓可分解为球谐能量，恢复多种球面摘要。实验表明该方法能检测到传统方法遗漏的复杂结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为流形上的加权点过程提供了结合几何信息的有效多尺度分析工具，尤其适用于球面数据分析中检测传统方法无法捕捉的复杂模式。

Abstract: Weighted empirical measures on compact manifolds arise in importance sampling, particle approximations, posterior summaries, quadrature, and representation learning. Standard weight-only summaries, such as ordinary effective sample size, ignore the geometry of the support. We introduce heat-kernel entropy profiles, a multiscale summary that diffuses weighted atoms by intrinsic heat flow and tracks nonuniformity across scales. For order-two Rényi entropy, the profile is computable from pairwise heat-kernel overlaps and yields a geometric effective sample size that discounts nearby or duplicate particles while matching ordinary effective sample size for well-separated particles. We prove monotonicity, small- and large-scale asymptotics, deterministic-weight consistency, and a bounded-ratio self-normalized importance-sampling extension for compact manifolds without boundary. On spheres, the unlogged profile decomposes into spherical-harmonic energies that recover mean-direction, von Mises-Fisher-type, and Bingham-type summaries. Sphere-based experiments show that the profile reveals antipodal, girdle, multimodal, and duplicate-particle structure missed by weight-only and first-moment spherical summaries.

</details>


### [160] [Tensor Train Diffusion: Leveraging Low-Rank Structures for High-Dimensional Score-Based Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06841)
*Robert Gruhlke,Julius Berner,David Sommer,Lorenz Richter*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于函数张量列（FTT）格式的高效求解器，用于扩散模型中逆向随机微分方程对应的HJB型偏微分方程，通过利用潜在低秩结构实现高维函数的模型压缩与快速计算，结合反向随机微分方程的逆向迭代方案，开发出快速、鲁棒且精确的采样方法，克服了现有方法训练时间长、超参数敏感等瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型求解时间逆向随机微分方程的方法（如PINNs或基于轨迹的技术）存在训练时间长、对超参数调优高度敏感等问题，亟需一种更高效、鲁棒且精确的求解方案。

Method: 采用函数张量列（FTT）格式利用潜在低秩结构高效逼近高维函数，实现模型压缩与快速计算；将该高效表示与基于反向随机微分方程（BSDEs）的逆向迭代方案相结合。

Result: 该方法克服了现有技术的主要瓶颈，能够以更高的效率从具有挑战性的目标分布中进行高保真采样。

Conclusion: 基于FTT格式的求解器为扩散模型中的HJB方程提供了一种快速、鲁棒且精确的替代方案，显著提升了采样效率与保真度。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer a powerful framework for sampling from complex probability densities by learning to reverse a noising process. A common approach involves solving for the time-reversed stochastic differential equation (SDE), which requires the score function of the evolving sample distribution. The logarithm of this distribution's density is governed by a Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) type partial differential equation (PDE). However, current methods for solving this PDE, such as PINNs or trajectory-based techniques, often suffer from long training times and significant sensitivity to hyperparameter tuning. In this work, we introduce a novel and efficient solver for the underlying HJB equation based on the functional tensor train (FTT) format. The FTT representation leverages latent low-rank structures to efficiently approximate high-dimensional functions, enabling both model compression and rapid computation. By integrating this efficient representation with a backward-in-time iterative scheme derived from backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs), we develop a fast, robust and accurate sampling method. Our approach overcomes primary bottlenecks of existing techniques, enabling high-fidelity sampling from challenging target distributions with improved efficiency.

</details>


### [161] [Finding a stationary point of a stochastic convex problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06883)
*Felipe Areces,John Duchi,Malo Sommers*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究随机凸优化中寻找驻点的问题，提出了更强的驻点判别准则（次微分包含小元素），并利用维度理论分解凸函数次微分图，证明了随机采样能保持图结构，从而有效应用近端点方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机凸优化方法通常使用代理准则（如邻近性保证或Moreau包络的小梯度）来近似驻点，但这些准则较弱。作者希望建立更强的驻点概念——即目标函数的次微分确实包含一个小元素，这在凸分析中是一个非平凡的问题，因为凸函数的次微分即使在最优点的任意小邻域内也无法一致收敛。

Method: 利用维度理论（dimension theory）对凸函数的次微分图进行分解，证明随机采样能够保留这些图的部分结构，并在此基础上有效应用近端点类方法（proximal-point-like methods）。

Result: 成功建立了在随机凸优化中寻找强意义下驻点的收敛保证，克服了次微分不连续收敛的困难。

Conclusion: 维度理论为分析凸函数次微分的结构提供了有效工具，结合随机采样与近端点方法，可以实现对强驻点准则的求解。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding stationary points for stochastic convex optimization problems. Rather than surrogates to stationarity, such as a proximity-to-stationarity guarantee or small gradient of the Moreau envelope, we ask for a stronger notion: that the subdifferential of the objective actually contains a small element. This criterion is non-trivial, because subdifferentials of convex functions fail to converge uniformly, even in arbitrarily small neighborhoods of the optimum. Our convergence guarantees rely on dimension theory to decompose the graph of the subdifferential of a convex function, showing how stochastic sampling preserves "pieces" of these graphs, and allowing effective application of proximal-point-like methods.

</details>


### [162] [Tensorized algorithms and scalable filtering methods for hidden Markov and factorial hidden Markov models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07008)
*Roxana Barrios,Ioannis Sgouralis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用张量代数直接处理因子隐马尔可夫模型（fHMM）的多维结构，无需构建等效但状态空间巨大的HMM，从而显著提升前向滤波算法的计算效率，使fHMM可用于大规模系统和数据密集型应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子隐马尔可夫模型（fHMM）虽然能更自然地表示受多个独立因素影响的真实系统，但等效为HMM后状态空间急剧膨胀，导致前向滤波等核心算法计算成本过高，限制了fHMM在大规模系统中的应用。

Method: 利用张量代数直接对fHMM的多维结构进行建模与计算，避免构建中间HMM表示，从而在原始因子空间中高效执行前向滤波算法。

Result: 所提出的张量化前向滤波方法显著提升了计算性能，能够高效分析大规模系统和大数据集，扩展了fHMM的适用边界。

Conclusion: 张量代数方法为fHMM提供了一种实用且可扩展的框架，使其能够有效应用于数据密集型场景，解决了传统HMM等价转换带来的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: A common method for the representation and analysis of time-series data is the hidden Markov model (HMM), where each observation is associated with a hidden state that evolves over time. However, many real-world systems are influenced by multiple independent factors, which are more naturally represented by factorial hidden Markov models (fHMM), where several hidden Markov chains jointly generate the observed data. Although an fHMM provides a richer and more realistic representation of many real-world systems, it can be reformulated as an equivalent HMM, but with a significantly larger state-space, leading to a severe increase in computational cost. In particular, the forward filtering algorithm, which is central to evaluation, decoding, and estimation tasks, becomes prohibitively expensive even for small systems. This work focuses on developing scalable methods for time-series analysis using tensor algebra to exploit the multidimensional structure of fHMM directly, without constructing intermediate HMM representations. Our novel filtering approach significantly improves computational performance and enables the efficient analysis of large systems and datasets, extending the scope of fHMM and providing a practical framework for data intensive applications.

</details>


### [163] [DiPhon: Diffusion on Graphons for Scalable Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07232)
*Sergio Rozada,Yiming Qin,Manuel Madeira,Pascal Frossard,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DiPhon，一种基于图极限对象（graphon）的扩散框架，用于可规模扩展的稠密图生成。通过Jacobi随机微分方程在graphon空间上定义连续扩散过程，并推导出可解析的边际得分，实现从小图训练到任意规模大图生成的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图生成任务中表现优异，但现有方法难以扩展到大规模图。作者希望通过图极限理论（graphon）弥合小图与大图生成之间的尺度鸿沟，实现无需重训练的尺寸可扩展图生成。

Method: 1）在graphon空间上定义Jacobi SDE作为连续扩散过程；2）提出DiPhon，即该连续过程的离散化图级近似；3）推导逆时过程，发现Jacobi过程的边际得分具有解析形式；4）通过图去噪网络估计得分，代入逆过程生成样本；5）理论证明了DiPhon精确匹配一阶矩，二阶矩误差有闭式表达。

Result: 理论证明DiPhon继承graphon动力学的尺寸无关统计特性（一阶矩精确匹配，二阶矩近似可控）。实验上，在小图上训练后可在推理时生成更大规模的图，且无需重训练，核心拓扑性质得以保持。

Conclusion: DiPhon通过将扩散过程建立在graphon空间上，提供了一种理论上有原则、实践上可扩展的图生成方法，为大规模图生成开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models represent a leading paradigm for graph generation, with notable impact in domains such as molecular design. Yet, scaling these models to large graphs remains an open problem. We approach this question in the dense-graph setting through the lens of graphons, the size-agnostic limit objects of dense graph sequences, to study how structural graph statistics behave across node-size scales. This perspective leads to DiPhon, a diffusion framework for size-scalable graph generation. Specifically, we formulate a continuous diffusion process on the graphon space via a Jacobi stochastic differential equation (SDE), and propose DiPhon, a discretized graph-level process that mimics these dynamics on finite graphs. We further derive the corresponding reverse-time process, which requires access to the marginal score. For the Jacobi process, this score interestingly admits a tractable form, which we estimate from data via graph denoising and plug into the reverse process to generate graph samples. We prove that DiPhon matches exactly the first moment of the marginal distributions induced by the continuous graphon process, and approximates the second moment up to a closed-form discrepancy. Thus, DiPhon inherits key size-agnostic statistical properties of the graphon dynamics, providing a principled route toward scalable graph generation. Empirically, we demonstrate this scalability by training on small graphs and generating progressively larger graphs at inference time, without retraining, while preserving their core topological properties.

</details>


### [164] [Statistical inverse learning and $\ell^1$-regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07468)
*Abhishake Rastogi,Tatiana A. Bubba,Tapio Helin,Luca Ratti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了统计逆学习框架下从有限、含噪、间接观测中恢复稀疏函数的问题。提出了ℓ1正则化经验风险最小化器，建立了几乎必然一致性和非渐近高概率收敛速率（预测范数和ℓ1重建范数），并证明这些速率在极小极大意义下是最优的。通过变分源条件和有效维数指数刻画收敛速率，并针对两类代表性逆问题（椭圆PDE中的反应系数识别和稀疏CT）验证了假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统计逆学习框架下，从有限、含噪、间接观测中恢复稀疏函数，并建立ℓ1正则化方法的理论基础，填补该领域在非渐近收敛速率和最优性方面的理论空白。

Method: 提出ℓ1正则化经验风险最小化器；利用变分源条件（光滑参数r）和协方差算子多项式谱衰减（有效维数指数b）刻画收敛速率；采用几乎必然一致性和非渐近高概率分析；推导极小极大下界证明最优性；通过合成算子分解和平滑算子分析验证源条件；针对滤波Radon变换推导有效维数渐近性。

Result: 建立了预测范数和ℓ1重建范数下的几乎必然一致性和非渐近高概率收敛速率；证明了匹配的极小极大下界，表明速率最优；证明了近似空间k_t中的成员等价于最佳n项逼近误差的多项式衰减；在椭圆PDE反应系数识别和稀疏CT两个逆问题中验证了假设；为滤波Radon变换导出了显式的有效维数渐近和具体收敛速率。

Conclusion: ℓ1正则化经验风险最小化器在统计逆学习框架下对稀疏函数恢复具有最优的收敛性质；理论结果与实际问题（如椭圆PDE和稀疏CT）的稀疏模型一致，为该类方法提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the recovery of sparse functions from finite, noisy, and indirect observations in the framework of statistical inverse learning. The unknown is modeled as an element of $\ell^1$, and observations are generated through a possibly nonlinear forward operator $A:\ell^1\to H$, where $H$ is a vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert space. We propose an $\ell^1$-regularized empirical risk minimizer and develop a theoretical analysis of its statistical properties.
  Under mild assumptions, we establish almost-sure consistency and derive non-asymptotic high-probability convergence rates in both the prediction and $\ell^1$ reconstruction norms. The rates depend on the source smoothness parameter $r$, characterized by a variational source condition, and the effective dimension exponent $b$, describing the polynomial spectral decay of the covariance operator. We further prove matching minimax lower bounds, showing that the obtained convergence rates are optimal.
  To relate the theory to practical sparsity models, we consider finitely smoothing operators of the form $A=G\circ S$, where $S$ is a synthesis operator, and show that approximation-space assumptions imply the required variational source conditions. In particular, we prove that membership in the approximation space $k_t$ is equivalent to polynomial decay of the best $n$-term approximation error.
  Finally, we verify the assumptions for two representative inverse problems: reaction coefficient identification in elliptic PDEs and sparse computed tomography. For filtered Radon transforms, we derive explicit effective-dimension asymptotics, yielding concrete convergence rates for standard image models and sparsifying systems.

</details>


### [165] [A Unified Detection Framework for AI-Related Content and Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07527)
*Xifeng Zhang,Tao Hu,Yijie Peng,Wan Tian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于马氏距离得分（MDS）的统一检测框架，用于检测LLM生成文本、幻觉、水印和对抗样本。通过联合估计方法改进协方差矩阵估计，并证明了算法收敛性与高崩溃点性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术广泛应用带来监管需求，检测AI相关内容是最直接经济的手段，现有方法缺乏统一框架。

Method: 基于马氏距离得分（MDS）的统一检测框架；使用casewise和cellwise最小协方差行列式（MCD）估计器的联合估计方法，以准确刻画正类（如人类生成文本、事实陈述等）的协方差矩阵；提供高效优化算法并证明收敛性；定义了联合估计器的崩溃点并证明高崩溃点性质。

Result: 实证评估证实了所提检测框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架可有效检测多种AI相关内容，具有高效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is a double-edged sword: while it has achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, its deployment also calls for effective oversight and regulation, for which the detection of AI-related content and artifacts is perhaps the most direct and cost-effective approach. To this end, we propose a unified detection framework based on Mahalanobis distance scores (MDS), applicable to several important settings, including the detection of large language model (LLM) generated text, hallucination, watermark, and adversarial examples. A key component of the proposed method is to accurately characterize the positive class--such as human-generated text, factual statements, unwatermarked text, or non-adversarial samples--which requires an efficient and robust estimator of the covariance matrix of deep representations of positive samples before computing the MDS. Since the positive samples typically consist of multiple classes, and these classes may exhibit both homogeneity and heterogeneity, we develop joint estimation methods for both the casewise and cellwise minimum covariance determinant (MCD) estimators. We provide efficient optimization algorithms for both estimators and prove their convergence. We provide a reasonable definition of the breakdown point for the joint estimators and prove their corresponding high breakdown point properties. Empirical evaluations confirm the effectiveness of the proposed detection framework.

</details>
